### [1] [Optimize, Learn, Refine: Whole-Body Grasping and Pick-and-Throw with a Spiral Soft Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38202)
*Marwah Basuhai,Tingcong Liu,Ibrahim Alsarraj,Yuhao Wang,Ke Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过基于结果的驱动空间优化，实现软连续体机器人从非抓取状态的全身抓取与拾取投掷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软连续体机器人可利用分布式柔顺性进行全身操作，但通过改变接触来合成行为仍然困难。

Method: 将抓取量化为尖端角扫掠和躯体-物体包裹，投掷进一步引入释放方向对齐和最小释放速度；由于驱动-结果映射非光滑，采用无导数CMA-ES并结合"优化-学习-精炼"框架：CMA-ES生成采样条件解，任务条件预测器学习热启动，再对未见条件精炼。

Result: 仿真中达到492/500成功抓取（98.4%），三个方向投掷成功率98%、97%、94%；学习初始化将抓取成功率从78.6%提升至98.4%，中位rollout次数从1184降至816；硬件实验中50次执行抓取成功率100%，30次拾取投掷成功率100%（每方向10次重复）。

Conclusion: 仿真与硬件结果共同证明了所提框架在虚拟和物理全身操作任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Soft continuum robots can exploit distributed compliance for whole-body manipulation, but synthesizing behavior through changing contacts remains difficult. We address whole-body grasping and pick-and-throw from an initially ungrasped state through outcome-based actuation-space optimization. Grasping is quantified by tip angular sweep and body-object enclosure, while throwing further incorporates release-direction alignment and minimum release speed. These objectives allow grasping, acceleration, and release to emerge from compliant interaction without prescribing contact forces, contact locations, or body configurations. Because the resulting actuation-to-outcome mapping is nonsmooth, we utilize derivative-free CMA-ES within an optimize-learn-refine framework. CMA-ES generates solutions for sampled conditions, a task-conditioned predictor learns warm starts, and CMA-ES refines them for unseen conditions. In simulation, the method achieves 492/500 successful grasps (98.4%) and success rates of 98%, 97%, and 94% across three directional throwing trials. Learned initialization increases grasping success from 78.6% to 98.4% while reducing the median rollout count from 1184 to 816 in CMA-ES. Hardware experiments achieve a 100% grasping success rate across 50 executions and a 100% pick-and-throw success rate across 30 executions, with 10 repetitions per direction. Together, these simulation and hardware results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework across both simulated and physical whole-body manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [2] [Fiatlux: A Long-Horizon Benchmark for Humanoid Ladder Climbing and Light-Bulb Replacement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38216)
*Pavel Bushuyeu,Yujin Chen,Anton Nikolaev,Brian Shu,Igor Molybog*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Fiatlux，一个基于NVIDIA Isaac Lab的灯泡更换长时域基准，让Unitree G1人形机器人完成搬梯子、攀爬、换灯泡与处置废灯泡的完整任务，并分解为十二个子任务评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常把桌面操作、平地家务或人形运动与操作分开评估，缺少在单个长时域回合中同时考察垂直移动、灵巧作业与易碎负载的评测，因此需要一个新的综合基准。

Method: 基于NVIDIA Isaac Lab构建Fiatlux基准，在单个回合中要求Unitree G1将梯子放到天花板或墙壁灯具下、攀爬、用新灯泡替换旧灯泡并将旧灯泡放入回收箱；将回合分解为十二个子任务环境，采用难度加权门控评分，允许部分得分；观测分为标准模式（物理机器人可感知或估计的信号）与特权模式（精确仿真状态）；提供评估协议及RSL-RL PPO、零样本NVIDIA GR00T N1.7 VLA模型和全身控制器的参考基线，并提供用于定义和检查成功门控的遥操作记录。

Result: 给出了Fiatlux的评估协议与多种参考基线实现，并提供遥操作记录用于成功门控的规格与验证；基准代码与遥操作记录已公开。

Conclusion: Fiatlux为垂直移动与易碎负载灵巧操作统一评估提供了长时域基准，代码与数据公开，便于后续研究与复现。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks evaluate tabletop manipulation, flat-floor household activity, or humanoid locomotion and manipulation as separate task groups; none scores vertical mobility and dexterous work on a fragile payload in one long-horizon episode. We present Fiatlux, a light-bulb replacement benchmark built on NVIDIA Isaac Lab. In one episode, a Unitree G1 humanoid positions a step ladder under a ceiling or wall fixture, climbs it, exchanges a spent bulb in a socket for a fresh one, and leaves the spent one in a disposal crate. We decompose the episode into twelve subtask environments scored on difficulty-weighted gates. The goal is a successful replacement, with the fresh bulb seated, the spent one disposed of, neither dropped, and a fragility bound not crossed. Runs that fall short can earn partial credit. Observations are split into a standard mode (signals a physical robot could sense or estimate) and a privileged mode (exact simulator state). We specify the evaluation protocol and provide reference baseline implementations spanning RSL-RL PPO, zero-shot NVIDIA GR00T N1.7 Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, and whole-body controllers. Additionally, we provide the teleoperated recordings used to specify and check the success gates. The benchmark code and the teleoperated recordings are available at fiatlux-bench.github.io.

</details>


### [3] [SynIL: Leveraging Synergy for Offline Imitation Learning from Imperfect Demonstration Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38225)
*Yuto Tanaka,Kyo Kutsuzawa,Martina Doku,Dai Owaki,Mitsuhiro Hayashibe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 SynIL 框架，基于神经科学中"运动协同"（motor synergy）与运动熟练度相关的证据，算法化量化协同表现，通过自监督奖励回归生成密集的转移级奖励信号，实现自动化、无标签的示范质量评估，用于离线强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习能从大规模示范数据中直接获取复杂技能，但当数据被次优或噪声示范污染时性能严重下降。现有质量评估方法依赖人工预选专家参考数据或任务特定启发式规则，限制了可扩展性。

Method: SynIL（Synergy-based Imitation Learning）：基于运动协同（movement 中低维协调结构）与运动熟练度相关的神经科学证据，算法化地量化协同的显现程度，并通过自监督奖励回归生成密集、转移级的奖励信号。

Result: 在 D4RL 运动基准与多人类 Robomimic 操作数据集上的综合评估表明，协同导出的奖励与真实奖励强相关；SynIL 显著优于行为克隆（BC），性能与使用真实环境奖励训练的离线强化学习相当，在稀疏奖励的人类遥操作场景中甚至更优。

Conclusion: SynIL 提供了一种自动化、无需标签的示范质量评估方案，克服了对人工专家参考数据和任务特定启发式的依赖，提升了可扩展性与在受污染数据下的模仿学习性能。

Abstract: Imitation learning enables robots to acquire complex skills directly from massive demonstration datasets, but its performance degrades severely when datasets are contaminated with suboptimal or noisy demonstrations. While prior quality-assessment methods attempt to filter or reweight data, they typically rely on manual pre-selection of expert reference data or task-specific heuristics, limiting scalability. To address this challenge, we introduce SynIL (Synergy-based Imitation Learning), a novel framework for automated, label-free demonstration quality assessment in offline reinforcement learning. Grounded in neuroscientific evidence that motor synergy, a low-dimensional coordinated structure in movement, correlates directly with motor proficiency, SynIL algorithmically quantifies synergy manifestation to generate dense, transition-level reward signals via self-supervised reward regression. Comprehensive evaluations on D4RL locomotion benchmarks and multi-human Robomimic manipulation datasets demonstrate that synergy-derived rewards correlate strongly with ground-truth rewards. Furthermore, SynIL substantially outperforms Behavior Cloning (BC) and achieves performance comparable to, and in sparse-reward human teleoperation scenarios, superior to, offline reinforcement learning trained on true environment rewards.

</details>


### [4] [A Two-Echelon Covering Tour Vehicle Routing Problem with Drones for Post-Disaster Relief](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38227)
*Dang Viet Anh Nguyen,Rajesh Piplani,Aldy Gunawan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出两梯队覆盖巡回车辆路径问题（2E-CTVRP）用于灾后救援物资配送，建立混合整数线性规划模型，并提出GRASP-ILS-PR混合元启发式算法求解，在110个新基准算例上验证了其高效性与优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后救援物资配送需要快速、公平地到达受灾点。传统单梯队车辆路径难以兼顾卡车与无人机协同、卫星选址与路径规划的耦合，因此需要构建两梯队卡车-无人机协同配送模型并设计高效求解方法。

Method: 将问题建模为混合整数线性规划（MILP）。第一梯队中卡车从中心仓库运送物资与无人机至受灾区域边缘的卫星点；第二梯队中无人机从卫星点并行出发，配送至由受灾点聚类得到的聚类质心，卡车在卫星点等待无人机返回。问题综合了卫星-卡车分配、卡车路径排序和聚类-卫星分配。提出GRASP-ILS-PR混合元启发式，结合贪心随机构造、单轨迹搜索，以及针对聚类-卫星分配的周期性路径重连。

Result: 在110个新算例上，GRASP-ILS-PR在30秒内找到所有已知最优解；在多数较大算例上匹配或超越商业求解器1小时的解；在50个卫星点的算例上最多改进11%。算法明显优于传统GRASP加进化路径重连，且比使用相同搜索组件的模拟退火算法结果更稳定。车队配置分析表明：少数大型卡车在多数算例中最小化累积到达时间，而多辆小型卡车在每个算例中都能更早、更公平地送达物资。

Conclusion: 2E-CTVRP模型有效刻画了灾后两梯队卡车-无人机协同配送问题，GRASP-ILS-PR算法具有高效性与稳定性；车队配置需在累积到达时间最小化与配送公平性/及时性之间权衡。

Abstract: We introduce the two-echelon covering tour vehicle routing problem (2E-CTVRP) for the distribution of relief supplies after a disaster. In the first echelon, a fleet of trucks transports supplies and drones from a central depot to satellites at the periphery of the affected area. In the second echelon, drones launched in parallel from the satellites deliver the supplies to the centroids of victim clusters, which are obtained by clustering the victim locations, and each truck waits at a satellite until its drones have returned. The problem combines the assignment of satellites to trucks, the sequencing of the truck routes, and the assignment of clusters to satellites, and minimizes the sum of the arrival times of the trucks at the satellites and at the depot. We formulate the 2E-CTVRP as a mixed integer linear program and propose a hybrid metaheuristic, GRASP-ILS-PR, which combines greedy randomized construction, a single-trajectory search, and periodic path relinking on the assignment of clusters to satellites. On a new benchmark set of 110 instances, GRASP-ILS-PR finds all known optimal solutions within 30 seconds, matches or improves on the one-hour solutions of a commercial solver on most larger instances, and improves on them by up to 11% for instances with 50 satellites. It clearly outperforms a conventional GRASP with evolutionary path relinking and yields more consistent results than a simulated annealing algorithm that uses the same search components. An analysis of two fleet configurations shows that few large trucks minimize the cumulative arrival time in most instances, whereas many small trucks deliver supplies to the victims earlier and more equitably in every instance.

</details>


### [5] [TALK-Dem: Benchmarking Embodied Task Planning under Dementia-Associated Communication Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38371)
*Guangxin Zhao,Yiran Hu,Yuan Cao,Chenxi Jiang,Jianfei Yang,Yegang Du,Yasuyuki Taki,Yoshifumi Kitamura,Lin Gu,Zhi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TALK-Dem基准，用于评测LLM驱动的机器人任务规划在痴呆相关言语沟通下的鲁棒性，并提出CARE方法缓解性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM机器人规划器假设用户指令清晰、完整且聚焦任务，但真实用户（尤其是痴呆症患者）的沟通方式会导致规划错误甚至物理安全风险。

Method: 构建包含4800条指令的TALK-Dem基准，覆盖五种沟通模式（指代不精确、物体替代、空语、话题漂移、侵入）及三个强度级别；在六个开源LLM上评测，并提出Context-Aware Retrieval from Experience（CARE）方法，通过检索相关已解决任务提供任务特定解释和规划上下文。

Result: 相比理想指令，开源模型在各沟通模式下性能下降最高达22.3个百分点；CARE在六个开源模型上普遍优于标准提示基线，平均任务成功率提升18.1个百分点（相对vanilla prompt）。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了评估沟通鲁棒性以及为本地可部署辅助机器人开发有效适应策略的重要性，TALK-Dem数据集已公开。

Abstract: Existing LLM-driven robot task planners rely on a taken-for-granted assumption of an ideal user whose instructions are clear, complete, and task-focused. However, when interacting with real-world users, especially those experiencing cognitive impairments, such as people living with dementia (PLWD), the planners often make mistakes and even pose physical safety risks. We proposed TALK-Dem (Talking Attributes and Linguistic Knowledge in Dementia), the first benchmark for evaluating LLM-driven robot task planning under dementia-associated verbal communication. TALK-Dem contains 4,800 instructions and covers five typical communication patterns, including Referential Imprecision, Object Substitution, Empty Speech, Topic Drift, and Intrusion, at three intensity levels. Experiments across six open-weight LLMs reveal a substantial robustness gap. Across communication patterns, open-weight models exhibited performance drops of up to 22.3 percentage points compared to ideal instructions. This revealed a critical gap and even danger for real-world applications, especially in assistive robotics, where locally deployable models are necessary due to privacy concerns and connectivity constraints. To mitigate this issue, we proposed the Context-Aware Retrieval from Experience (CARE) method, which retrieves relevant previously resolved tasks to provide task-specific interpretation and planning context. CARE generally outperformed standard prompting baselines across the six open-weight models, improving average task success by 18.1 percentage points over the vanilla prompt. These results highlighted the importance of both evaluating communication robustness and developing effective adaptation strategies for locally deployable assistive robots. The TALK-Dem dataset is publicly available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TALK-Dem-A6B3/.

</details>


### [6] [GestAdapt: Workspace-Conditioned Co-Speech Gesture Generation for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38400)
*Bosong Ding,Xianglin Zhang,Miao Xin,Murat Kirtay,Giacomo Spigler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 GestAdapt，一个以工作空间为条件的共语手势生成框架，使机器人生成的手势能同时适应语音、机体形态与可用工作空间，而非事后修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人共语手势不仅要适应语音和机体，还需适应可用的运动工作空间。同一句话可配不同手势，机器人应针对空间约束（如靠墙讲话）生成合适动作，而不是先做无约束手势再修正，因为事后修正会损害手势自然度。

Method: GestAdapt 将共语手势生成以预设的手腕工作空间为条件。框架通过共享运动表示，从六个互补的共语语料库中学习，并支持重定向到不同机器人机体。生成时直接满足工作空间约束。

Result: 定量评估显示生成动作既贴近真实动作分布又满足工作空间约束。用户研究中，修改工作空间约束下的手势平均质量得分为 3.24/5，高于无工作空间变体（2.43/5），低于参考动作（3.68/5）。真实机器人评估中，所有动作在相同约束下重定向到 Reachy2 人形机器人，本框架生成动作在 69.7% 的对比中排名第一，优于无工作空间基线及事后约束的参考动作。

Conclusion: 结果支持在生成过程中就让手势适应可用工作空间，而非事后修改无约束轨迹来满足约束，从而避免牺牲手势自然度。

Abstract: Co-speech gestures for robots must adapt not only to speech and embodiment, but also to the workspace available for performing the motion. Since the same speech can be accompanied by different gestures, a robot can respond to workspace constraints, e.g., gestures for speech next to a wall. In these scenarios, the robot should gesture in a suitable motion rather than simply correcting an unconstrained one. To achieve this goal, we present GestAdapt, a workspace-conditioned framework that conditions co-speech gesture generation on a prescribed wrist workspace. The GestAdapt framework learns from six complementary co-speech corpora through a shared motion representation and supports retargeting to different robot embodiments. Quantitative evaluation shows that generated motions remain close to the real-motion distribution while respecting the workspace. In a user study, gestures generated under modified workspace constraints receive a mean quality score of 3.24/5, above our no-workspace variant (2.43/5) and below the reference motions (3.68/5). In a real robot evaluation, all compared motions are retargeted to the Reachy2 humanoid robot under identical workspace constraints. Motions generated with our framework rank first in 69.7\% of comparisons, higher than our no-workspace variant baseline and retargeted ground-truth motions constrained afterward. Overall, the results support adapting gestures to the available workspace during generation, rather than modifying unconstrained trajectories afterward to satisfy workspace constraints, potentially compromising gesture naturalness.

</details>


### [7] [Memorize, Adapt, Ignore: Diagnosing Robot Learning Mechanisms under Training Data Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38401)
*Ke Zhang,Danica J. Sutherland,Chao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文通过经验神经正切核（NTK）系统研究训练数据变化（域随机化、示范数据多样化）对机器人操作策略鲁棒性的影响机制，发现NTK可区分"记忆"与"适应"，并据此提出数据变化设计的实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据变化（如仿真中的域随机化方案设计、模仿学习的示范数据整理）是提升机器人操作策略鲁棒性的主要手段，但其底层机制尚不清楚，实践者通常只能通过昂贵的试错来选择随机化参数。作者希望揭示这些机制，将数据变化设计从经验试错转变为有理论诊断依据的工程实践。

Method: 作者开展一系列案例研究，在多个设置下对物体尺寸、颜色、类型以及场景光照、语言提示进行随机化：包括ManiSkill中的抓取放置强化学习，以及在LIBERO和RoboTwin上微调视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型。研究同时考察模型行为与内部表示，并以经验神经正切核（NTK）作为主要诊断工具，还引入了基于NTK的信噪比指标，用于判断策略是否学会了忽略任务无关因素；随后将诊断结果用于提出DR方案设计、模型选择与捷径学习检测的实用建议，并比较不同表示类型，最后用基于ACT的模仿学习在真实硬件上验证结论。

Result: NTK能够区分内部学习机制的转变：在变化不足时，策略倾向于"记忆"不同情形（如分别为大立方体和小立方体学习该做什么）；而在变化充分时，策略转为"适应"当前情境。基于NTK的信噪比还能识别出策略是否学会了忽略任务无关因素（如把蓝、红立方体等同处理，而非分别学习蓝色子策略与红色子策略）。相关发现还在真实硬件ACT模仿学习实验中得到验证。

Conclusion: 训练数据变化的作用机制可由NTK等内部表示诊断工具有效刻画，"记忆—适应"转变与任务无关因素的忽略均可被量化识别。这些诊断工具可为设计域随机化方案、选择模型以及检测捷径学习提供实用指导，并适用于不同类型的表示与真实机器人场景。

Abstract: Training data variation, whether through designing a domain randomization (DR) scheme in simulation or curating demonstrations for imitation learning, is a primary lever for improving the robustness of robotic manipulation policies. Yet its underlying mechanisms remain poorly understood, and practitioners typically select randomization parameters through expensive trial and error. We investigate these mechanisms through a series of case studies, randomizing object size, color, and type as well as scene lighting and linguistic prompts across settings including pick-and-place RL in ManiSkill and fine-tuning of vision-language-action (VLA) models on LIBERO and RoboTwin. We examine both model behavior and internal representations, using the empirical neural tangent kernel (NTK) as our primary diagnostic tool. We show that the NTK distinguishes a shift in the internal learning mechanism from \textit{memorizing} different situations with insufficient variation (e.g.\ learning what to do for a large cube, and what to do for a small cube) to \textit{adapting} to the situation at hand with sufficient variation. An NTK-based signal-to-noise ratio also helps distinguish when policies have learned to \emph{ignore} task-irrelevant factors (e.g.\ treating blue and red cubes identically, instead of learning a blue sub-policy and a red sub-policy). We use these diagnostics to develop practical guidance for designing DR schemes, selecting models, and detecting shortcut learning. We further compare different kinds of representations and validate our findings with real-world hardware experiments using ACT-based imitation learning.

</details>


### [8] [Draft: A Parametric Tool for Robot Design Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38405)
*David Nguyen,Marcelo Coelho,Sangbae Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Draft，一个参数化生成工具，可将任意串行链参数化树直接编译为可仿真的 MJCF 模型，无需 CAD，并利用 114 个执行器和 49 个已发表机器人描述拟合的趋势来锚定设计参数，最后通过四台现成机器人的数字孪生和三台四足机器人强化学习实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人性能常受限于形态与控制协同迭代的高成本，因为每次 CAD 改动都需先转换为可仿真模型才能开展控制工作。现有协同设计方法多针对单一平台编写模型生成器，或缺乏真实世界数据支撑设计合理性。

Method: Draft 是一个参数化生成工具，其通用引擎可将任意串行链参数化树直接编译成仿真就绪的 MJCF 模型，无需 CAD。用户可通过易调整的模型探索设计权衡，并评估设计变化对控制器性能的影响。Draft 利用对 114 个执行器和 49 个已发表机器人描述拟合的趋势来约束各设计的自由参数，使生成的机器人锚定于真实硬件。

Result: 通过构建四台现成机器人的数字孪生，其质量与真实值几何平均折叠误差为 1.10 倍，整体验证了所拟合趋势的准确性。此外，通过两阶段强化学习课程评估三台四足机器人，展示了 Draft 如何揭示设计权衡。

Conclusion: Draft 提供了一种无需 CAD、跨平台且基于真实硬件数据锚定的机器人形态与控制协同设计方式，能有效支撑设计权衡探索与控制器性能评估。

Abstract: Robot performance is often limited by the cost of iterating on morphology and control together, since every computer-aided design (CAD) change has to be carried into a simulation-ready model before control work begins. Co-design methods attempt to close this gap, but each uses a model generator written for a single platform or lack the use of real-world data to suggest that designs are plausible. We present Draft, a parametric generation tool whose generalized engine compiles any parametric tree of serial chains into a simulation-ready MJCF model, without CAD. It allows engineers to explore design tradeoffs through easily adjustable models and evaluate how changes influence controller performance. Draft grounds the free parameters of each design using trends fitted to a survey of $114$ actuators and $49$ published robot descriptions, so that a generated robot is anchored to real-world hardware. We validate those trends wholistically by building twins of four off-the-shelf robots, whose masses agree to $1.10\times$ geometric mean fold error. Finally, we demonstrate how Draft exposes design tradeoffs by evaluating three quadrupeds through a two-stage reinforcement learning curriculum.

</details>


### [9] [PneuTac: Tactile Manipulation with Soft Pneumatic Robots via Unified MPM-Gaussian Splatting Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38418)
*Shaohong Zhong,Marco Pontin,Joe Watson,Perla Maiolino,Ingmar Posner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PneuTac是一个统一框架，用MPM与3DGS联合建模软体气动机器人与触觉传感器，通过视觉化实到仿建模与代理模型训练，实现触觉反馈操作，并在真实任务中超越仅用真实数据的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体气动机器人可提供柔性安全接触，视触觉传感器具备高分辨率触觉感知，二者结合在精细操作中潜力巨大。但用柔性机器人学习触觉操作受限于仿真效率：现有模拟器通常将软体机器人与触觉传感器分开建模，且存在难以高效跨越的校准误差（sim-to-real gap）。

Method: 提出PneuTac统一框架：用物质点法（MPM）建模软体机器人与可变形触觉膜动力学，用3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）进行渲染；采用简单的基于视觉的方法完成实到仿（real-to-sim）建模；再用代理模型训练动作与感知网络以加速仿真；最后以触觉引导流程在仿真中采集示范数据。

Result: 在定制的气动软指（带触觉感知指尖）上实验，并进行跨设备评估，表明PneuTac能准确建模带触觉传感器的软体机器人；在三个真实高接触柔性操作任务中，用仿真增强示范训练的策略优于仅用同样真实数据训练的基线。

Conclusion: PneuTac是面向柔性硬件的实用触觉操作框架，能统一建模软体机器人与触觉传感并有效生成仿真增强示范。

Abstract: Soft robots and tactile sensors have demonstrated great potential in delicate manipulation tasks. Soft pneumatic robots enable safe contact through compliance, and vision-based tactile sensors offer high-resolution touch perception. However, learning tactile manipulation with compliant robots has been challenging, bottlenecked by the lack of efficient simulation. Existing simulators typically model them in isolation, and exhibit large calibration gaps that are difficult to overcome efficiently. We present PneuTac, a unified framework for tactile-feedback manipulation with soft pneumatic robots. We leverage the material point method (MPM) for modelling the dynamics of the soft robot and the deformable tactile membrane, and 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) for rendering. Real-to-sim modelling is done with a simple vision-based method, to then train action and perception networks for efficient simulation with surrogate models. We use the framework to drive a tactile-guided pipeline to collect demonstrations in simulation. Through experiments on a custom-designed pneumatic soft finger with a tactile sensing tip, together with additional cross-device evaluations, we show that PneuTac is capable of accurately modelling soft robots with tactile sensors, and that policies trained with simulation-augmented demonstrations outperform baselines trained on the same real data on three real-world contact-rich compliant manipulation tasks, making it a practical framework for tactile manipulation on compliant hardware.

</details>


### [10] [A Reachability-based Safety Certificate for Dynamical System Motion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38421)
*Aditya Vats,Tianyi Xia,Nadia Figueroa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于后向可达管的值函数，用于认证动态系统（DS）在未知环境中部署的安全性，并证明该值函数在稳定性条件下是良定义的、可通过确定性rollout计算，是标称DS流无障碍区域的最大前向不变子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DS作为具有稳定性与收敛保证的向量场运动策略，在未知环境部署时需局部重塑以保证安全，但现有调制或几何CBF策略在复杂障碍与DS下可能导致不可避免碰撞或伪吸引子；现有安全价值函数通常因维数灾难而难以求解。

Method: 从后向可达管概念出发构建价值函数，度量标称DS rollout轨迹上的最坏情况安全性；利用DS-based学习演示场景中无控制输入使可达性问题退化为确定性rollout，并借助稳定性条件将无限时域截断为有限时域，从而得到良定义价值函数；提出CBF-on-V方法。

Result: 证明该价值函数是标称DS流无障碍区域的最大前向不变子集；在解析、Neural ODE、微分同胚潜空间、LPV-DS、SE(3)五种DS构建上验证，并在Franka机械臂上实验；表明调制与几何CBF在正对不安全区域时存在鞍点问题，而CBF-on-V完全避免该陷阱。

Conclusion: 所提值函数为DS安全性提供了可计算且严格的认证，CBF-on-V方法在多种DS构建与机械臂实验中有效，并克服了现有局部策略的鞍点缺陷。

Abstract: Dynamical Systems (DS) are reactive motion policies representing vector fields trained with theoretical guarantees of stability and convergence. To ensure safety during deployment in unknown environments they must be locally reshaped, either through modulation or geometric control barrier function strategies. However, depending on the geometry of the obstacles and the complexity of the DS, these local strategies can lead the system to unavoidable collisions or spurious attractors. In this work, we certify safety with a value function drawn from the notion of backward reachability tube, which measures the worst-case safety along a rollout trajectory of the nominal DS. Usually, such a value function is intractable for a controlled system due to curse of dimensionality. We show that in the DS-based learning-from-demonstration setting, the absence of a control input collapses the reachability problem to a deterministic rollout, and the presence of certain stability conditions truncates the infinite horizon to a finite one, resulting in a well-defined value function. We further show that the value function we devised is the maximal forward-invariant subset of the obstaclefree region for the nominal DS flow. The application of this certificate function is validated across five DS constructions - analytical, Neural ODE, diffeomorphic latent space, LPV-DS, SE(3)and validate it on a Franka manipulator. Modulation and geometric CBFs also suffer from saddle point in cases of headon approach towards an unsafe zone. We show that CBF-on-V avoids this pitfall entirely.

</details>


### [11] [Embodiment-aware control by inference over the operator: a simulation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38437)
*Sara Falcone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出"通用具身引擎"(UEE)，一种具身感知控制器，通过隐式注视与瞳孔信号推断操作者的具身感与视觉-本体感觉线索权重，并将其建模为离散主动推断智能体；在300个异质合成操作者仿真中，UEE能在半分钟内找到合适设置并接近理想控制效果，其收益主要来自对操作者的贝叶斯推断与显式偏好，而非主动推断的信息寻求项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥操作系统以信道保真度为调优目标，而操作者是否将设备体验为身体一部分（具身感SoE）却只能在事后通过问卷测量。预测加工理论认为应控制设备以缩小预测与反馈间的失配，但预测本身不可观测，且仅惩罚失配的目标可通过移除反馈而被最小化，因此需要一种能在运行中客观推断并优化具身感的控制方法。

Method: 将控制问题形式化为具身感知控制器UEE：从隐式注视和瞳孔信号及任务结果中推断操作者的具身感与视觉-本体感觉线索加权，在显式偏好下选择有界的设备设置，并将整个框架实现为离散主动推断智能体。在300个异质合成操作者上进行仿真验证。

Result: UEE在大多数操作者中于半分钟内找到合适设置，甚至早于识别其确切类型，并在会话后半段接近理想控制器（具身感1.68，对比最佳固定设置的0.73，0-2量程）。不读取操作者的自适应控制与固定控制无差别，无模型多臂老虎机表现更差；而采用相同推断的期望效用控制器表现完全一致，说明收益来自对操作者的贝叶斯推断和显式偏好，而非主动推断的信息寻求项。朴素的预测误差最小化器按目标逻辑扣留了反馈，导致任务成功率下降（0.74 vs 0.90）。该优势在模型族之外的操作者上缩小但依然存在，并随群体多样性增大而增长，但当控制器信任无信息信号、或线索加权在会话中途变化而未被建模时，优势消失。

Conclusion: UEE证明了在运行中推断并优化具身感的可行性，但失效情形表明，以人为对象的后续研究必须首先建立两件事：经过校准的信号，以及对变化的建模。这些失败案例界定了从仿真走向真人实验的前提条件。

Abstract: Teleoperation systems are tuned for channel fidelity, while whether the operator experiences the device as part of the body, the Sense of Embodiment (SoE), is measured only afterwards, by questionnaire. Predictive-processing accounts suggest controlling devices to reduce the mismatch between the operator's predictions and the returned feedback, but those predictions are unobservable, and an objective that only penalizes mismatch is minimized by removing feedback. We formulate an embodiment-aware controller, the Universal Embodiment Engine (UEE), that infers the operator's embodiment and visuo-proprioceptive cue weighting from implicit gaze and pupil signals and task outcome, and chooses bounded device settings under explicit preferences, cast as a discrete Active Inference agent. In simulations with 300 heterogeneous synthetic operators, the UEE found the suitable setting within half a minute for most operators, before identifying their exact type, and came close to an oracle in the second half of the session (embodiment 1.68 against 0.73 for the best fixed setting, on a 0-2 scale). Adapting without reading the operator did no better than fixed control, and model-free bandits did worse, whereas an expected-utility controller with the same inference did exactly as well: the benefit comes from Bayesian inference over the operator with explicit preferences, not from the information-seeking term of Active Inference. A naive prediction-error minimizer withheld feedback, as its objective implies, and lost task success (0.74 vs 0.90). The benefit shrank but persisted for operators outside the controller's model family, grew with the variety of the population, and vanished when the controller trusted an uninformative signal or when cue weighting changed mid-session without being modeled. These failures show what studies with people must establish first: calibrated signals and a model of change.

</details>


### [12] [BIND: Binding 3D Robot Actions to 2D Image Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38443)
*Cameron Smith,Arsh Tangri,Vitor Guizilini,Yue Wang,Zubair Irshad,Sergey Zakharov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BIND 通过相机几何将 3D 机器人动作与 2D 图像特征绑定，显著提升视觉运动策略的数据效率与对分布外视角、物体位置的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略的动作头多采用 MLP 回归，从预训练视觉编码器输出的单一全局特征向量直接映射到动作，需要仅凭示范自行发现动作与图像特征之间的对应关系。虽然现代图像特征语义丰富、空间鲁棒且多视角一致，但由此构建的策略对相机视角和物体位置的细微变化十分脆弱，且数据效率出奇地低。

Method: BIND 用相机几何而非学习来提供动作—特征关系：对候选末端执行器位置体进行离散化，将每个候选位置与各相机视角下其投影处的预训练特征绑定，再通过打分每个候选的位置与其图像绑定特征的组合来选择动作。

Result: 在真实机器人上研究了数据效率、对未见物体位置与相机视角的分布外鲁棒性，以及长时序任务执行与灵巧性。BIND 数据效率高且鲁棒：在少至 5 个示范的任务上达到近乎完美的成功率，在相机视角剧烈偏移与留出物体位置下性能优雅退化，而坐标回归基线则完全失败。

Conclusion: 将 3D 动作与 2D 图像特征通过相机几何绑定，可替代基于全局特征的隐式学习式回归，从而获得高数据效率与强分布外鲁棒性的视觉运动机器人策略。

Abstract: We introduce BIND, a new action representation for visuomotor robot policies that binds 3D robot actions to their corresponding 2D image features, yielding strong data efficiency gains and robustness to out-of-distribution object positions and camera viewpoints. The action heads of current robot policies are typically formulated as an MLP regression from a single global feature vector produced by a pre-trained vision encoder. This global formulation requires the policy network to discover, from demonstrations alone, the relationship between target robot actions and the image features they project onto. The consequence is that although modern image features are semantically descriptive, spatially robust, and even multiview-consistent, the policies built on them are brittle to subtle changes in camera viewpoint and object placement--and surprisingly data-inefficient. BIND closes this gap by supplying the action-feature relationship through camera geometry rather than learning: it discretizes a volume of candidate end effector positions, attaches each candidate to the pre-trained features at its projection in each camera view, and selects actions by scoring each candidate's position and image-bound feature combination. On a real robot, we study data efficiency and out-of-distribution robustness to unseen object positions and camera viewpoints, as well as general long-horizon task execution and dexterity. We find BIND to be highly data-efficient and robust: it achieves near-perfect success on tasks with as few as 5 demonstrations, and degrades gracefully under steep camera-viewpoint shifts and held-out object positions where coordinate-regression baselines completely fail.

</details>


### [13] [Observability Analysis and Online Calibration of Visual-Inertial-Wheel Odometry for 4WIS4WID Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38462)
*Branimir Ćaran,Vladimir Milić,Bojan Šekoranja,Bojan Jerbić*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向四轮独立转向驱动(4WIS4WID)机器人的视觉-惯性-轮式里程计(VIWO)框架，支持在线标定，包含2D里程计模型、预积分与解析雅可比，并完成可观测性与退化运动分析，仿真和实车实验验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4WIS4WID机器人运动模式灵活，但传统轮式里程计模型难以直接适用，且转向偏移等轮内参需要在线标定；同时系统可观测性、退化运动及激励条件尚不清晰，需要专门建模与分析。

Method: 直接从四个驱动速度与转向角推导2D里程计模型，利用各轮的纵向滚动约束与横向无滑移约束；构建预积分模型与解析雅可比以实现高效滤波和标定；对线性化VIWO系统进行可观测性分析，并对零偏航率、恒定转向、车轮不滚动等退化运动及13个轮内参的激励条件进行推导。

Result: 可观测性分析表明：仅驱动模型下所有转向偏移不可观测，而所提出的冗余模型恢复了其可观测性；识别出四个标准VINS不可观测方向及三个由里程计参考系任意放置引入的额外方向；刻画了多种退化运动及其对应的激励条件。仿真与真实4WIS4WID机器人实验验证了系统性能。

Conclusion: 该VIWO框架通过冗余建模与在线标定，有效解决了4WIS4WID机器人里程计标定与可观测性问题，并为退化运动下的激励条件提供理论指导，实验证明其可行性与有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a visual-inertial-wheel odometry (VIWO) framework with online calibration for four-wheel independently steered and driven (4WIS4WID) mobile robots. We derive a 2D odometry model directly from the four driving velocities and steering angles, using both the longitudinal rolling constraints and the lateral no-slip constraints of all wheels. A preintegration model and analytical Jacobians are developed for efficient filtering and calibration. An observability analysis of the linearized VIWO system shows that a drive-only model makes all steering offsets unobservable, whereas the proposed redundant model restores their observability. The analysis also identifies four standard VINS unobservable directions and three additional directions associated with the arbitrary placement of the odometry reference frame. Furthermore, we characterize several degenerate motions, including zero yaw rate, constant steering, and a non-rolling wheel, and derive the corresponding excitation conditions for the thirteen wheel intrinsics that remain after fixing the odometry frame reference. The performance of the proposed system has been demonstrated in both simulation and real-world experiments on a 4WIS4WID mobile robot.

</details>


### [14] [TrafficSignBench: Rule-Centric Closed-Loop Evaluation of Traffic-Sign Compliance in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38463)
*Victoria Smirnova,Viktoriia Zinkovich,Gregorii Bukhtuev,Artem Belyaev,Andrey Kuznetsov,Denis Shepelev,Vlad Shakhuro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 TrafficSignBench，一个以交通标志为中心的大规模基准，用于系统且可解释地评估自动驾驶规划器的交通规则遵从性，并生成合规专家轨迹用于微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶规划器通常用驾驶分数、到达率、碰撞率等聚合指标评估，无法显式衡量交通规则遵从性，导致规划器可能在高分下仍存在不安全或违规行为，限制其实际部署。

Method: 结合基于真实地图的仿真与面向规则的程序化场景生成，实现可扩展且均衡的规则覆盖；对 34 种交通标志实现对应规则，并配备自动规则检查器以在闭环执行中检测违规。

Result: 构建了 29,000 个多样道路场景与 29 类测试场景，并挑选 5,800 个测试场景；实验表明当前规划器在标准指标上表现良好，但规则遵从性较差。

Conclusion: 通过显式交通标志约束将现有规划器转化为规则合规的轨迹专家，可扩展生成高质量专家轨迹用于微调，从而提升规则遵从性。

Abstract: Autonomous driving planners are typically evaluated using aggregate metrics such as driving score, destination rate, and collision rate, which do not explicitly measure compliance with traffic rules. As a result, planners can achieve high benchmark scores while still exhibiting unsafe or illegal behaviors, limiting their applicability to real-world deployment. To address this gap, we introduce TrafficSignBench, a large-scale, traffic sign-centric benchmark for systematic and interpretable evaluation of traffic-rule compliance in autonomous driving. Our framework combines real-map-based simulation for realistic road layouts with rule-targeted procedural scenario generation for scalable and balanced coverage of underrepresented rules. We implement traffic rules corresponding to 34 traffic signs, each equipped with an automatic rule checker for detecting violations during closed-loop execution. This design yields 29,000 diverse road scenes and 29 distinct testing scenario types, enabling controlled evaluation of rule-specific planner behavior. We construct 5,800 testing scenes and demonstrate that current autonomous driving planners can exhibit poor traffic-rule compliance despite strong performance on standard evaluation metrics. To address this limitation, we transform existing planners into rule-compliant trajectory experts via explicit traffic-sign constraints, enabling scalable generation of high-quality oracle trajectories for fine-tuning.

</details>


### [15] [L1-MPPI: L1 Adaptive Model Predictive Path Integral for Agile UAV Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38467)
*Lukáš Kotek,Ondřej Procházka,Vojtěch Vonásek,Martin Saska,Robert Pěnička*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出L1-MPPI，将L1自适应控制与MPPI级联，提升无人机高速轨迹跟踪在模型不确定性和外部扰动下的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向无人机控制的MPPI方法未显式建模气动效应与变化的载荷，且通常忽略底层电机控制器动态，导致高速跟踪精度受限。

Method: 将L1自适应控制与MPPI级联；在MPPI所用动力学模型中引入底层飞控与电机动态，并采用反映底层控制器方法的迭代混合方案。

Result: 仿真与实机均显示跟踪性能提升。35%质量增加飞行时，相较普通MPPI的RMSE降低58.61%；相较使用在线质量估计替代L1自适应的MPPI，RMSE降低38.59%。实机速度达13.50 m/s，加速度达2.5 g。

Conclusion: L1-MPPI在未知载荷等扰动下仍保持高精度跟踪，验证了级联自适应与改进动力学建模的有效性。

Abstract: This work proposes the L1 Adaptive Model Predictive Path Integral (L1-MPPI). It cascades L1 adaptive control with the Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) to improve tracking of high-speed UAV trajectories. Thanks to the L1augmentation, the tracking remains accurate even under model uncertainties and external disturbances, such as an additional payload or a mismatch in the modeled aerodynamic drag. In contrast to existing MPPI approaches for UAV control that do not explicitly model aerodynamic effects, varying payloads, and typically neglect the dynamics of low-level motor controllers, our L1-MPPI approach enhances the dynamic model used in the MPPI by incorporating the low-level flight controller and motor dynamics, as well as an iterative mixing scheme that reflects the approach of the low-level controller. The proposed method demonstrates improved tracking performance in both simulation and the real world, even when the UAV is subjected to an unknown payload. In flight with 35% mass increase, our approach lowers the RMSE by 58.61% with respect to plain MPPI. Compared to the same MPPI using an online mass estimator in place of the L1 augmentation, the RMSE is lower by 38.59%. During the real-world experiments the UAV reaches speeds up to 13.50 m/s and accelerations up to 2.5 g.

</details>


### [16] [Diffusion-2BC: Hybrid Diffusion and Regression Training for Offline Behavior Cloning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38472)
*Bruno Maciel Machado,Eric Aislan Antonelo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Diffusion-2BC，将扩散去噪目标与辅助确定性行为克隆损失结合、共享视觉编码器，辅助分支仅用于训练。在 Claw 与 CARLA 上均优于扩散行为克隆和确定性行为克隆基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆是自动驾驶策略学习的离线方案，但均方误差回归难以适配"同一观测对应多个有效动作"的演示数据；扩散策略虽能建模条件多模态动作分布，但在数据有限时视觉特征与控制的闭环表现可能不稳定。

Method: Diffusion-2BC：在共享视觉编码器上同时使用扩散去噪目标与辅助确定性行为克隆损失；辅助分支只在训练阶段使用，推理阶段保持纯扩散方式。

Result: Claw 任务中，平均掩码距离误差较扩散行为克隆基线降低约 10%，较标准确定性行为克隆降低 85%。无路线 CARLA 中，在 Town01 与 Town02 下均在终止前行驶显著更远。定性 rollout 呈现不同路线选择，体现多模态行为。

Conclusion: 辅助回归信号可提升扩散行为克隆的闭环可靠性，同时在受控基准中保留多模态预测能力。

Abstract: Behavior cloning provides an offline route to autonomous-driving policy learning, but mean-squared-error regression is poorly matched to demonstrations in which one observation admits several valid actions. Diffusion policies can represent conditional multimodal action distributions, yet their closed-loop performance may be unstable when visual features and control are learned from limited data. This paper presents Diffusion-2BC, which combines a diffusion denoising objective with an auxiliary deterministic behavior-cloning loss over a shared visual encoder. The auxiliary branch is used only during training; inference remains diffusion-based. The proposed method is evaluated in the controlled Claw environment and in bird's-eye-view CARLA navigation, including route-conditioned driving, route-free navigation through multiple intersections, and cross-map evaluation from Town01 to Town02. In the Claw task, Diffusion-2BC reduced the mean mask-distance error by approximately 10% relative to a diffusion-based behavior-cloning baseline and by 85% relative to standard deterministic behavior cloning. In route-free CARLA, Diffusion-2BC traveled substantially farther before termination under the evaluation protocol than both baselines in Town01 and Town02. Additional qualitative rollouts revealed distinct route choices, showing the multimodal behavior of the proposed diffusion-based agent. The results indicate that an auxiliary regression signal can improve the closed-loop reliability of diffusion behavior cloning while preserving multimodal prediction in the controlled benchmark.

</details>


### [17] [CADeT: Causal-Aware Deformation Transmission for Indirect Robotic Manipulation of Soft Tissue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38483)
*Junlei Hu,Dominic Jones,Pietro Valdastri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CADeT 因果感知形变传递框架，结合结构因果模型与主动感知，在线推断潜在传递模式并估计黏附雅可比，用于机器人微创手术中的间接形变控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人辅助微创手术中，机器人无法直接接触深层可形变解剖结构，中间组织会空间滤波形变传递；被动观测下"解耦模式"与"阻塞模式"运动响应相似，导致模式识别歧义。

Method: 构建结构因果模型（SCM）结合主动感知的 CADeT 框架：正常操作时用控制动作更新模式信念；歧义持续时选择额外探测动作提升模式可区分性；在线学习状态相关的黏附雅可比，并将模式信念与雅可比嵌入信念感知模型预测控制器（MPC）实现间接目标形状控制。

Result: 在仿真及 da Vinci 研究套件（dVRK）上，使用体模与离体猪组织验证，模式识别准确率更高，形状误差收敛速度快于所评估的无模型与基于模型的基线方法。

Conclusion: 在评估条件下，主动感知能够提升模式识别能力与间接形变控制性能。

Abstract: Indirect manipulation of deep-seated deformable anatomy inaccessible to the robot is challenging in robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery (RAMIS) because intervening tissues spatially filter deformation transmission. Passive observations can be ambiguous because the Decoupled and Blocked modes may produce similar motion responses. We propose CADeT, a causal-aware deformation transmission framework that integrates structural causal model (SCM) with active sensing to infer a latent transmission mode and estimate a state-dependent adhesion Jacobian online. During normal manipulation, control actions update the mode belief; when ambiguity persists, an additional probing action is selected to improve mode distinguishability. The mode belief and learned Jacobian are incorporated into a belief-aware model predictive controller for indirect target-shape control. Validation in simulation and on the da Vinci research kit (dVRK), using phantom and ex vivo porcine tissues, shows higher mode-identification accuracy and faster shape-error convergence than the evaluated model-free and model-based baselines. These results show that active sensing improves mode identification and indirect deformation control under the evaluated conditions.

</details>


### [18] [What to Attend, What to Keep: Skill-Conditioned Visuotactile Representation with Progress-Guided Event Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38494)
*Amir-Hossein Shahidzadeh,Seungjae Lee,Eadom Dessalene,Shanthosh Raaj Mohanram Mageswari,Soroush Etemad,Furong Huang,Cornelia Fermüller,Yiannis Aloimonos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种技能条件化的多模态表征，让查询到的技能动态调节视觉与触觉的融合，并引入稀疏事件记忆保留最近K个技能的终端观测，在接触密集型任务上显著降低检测延迟与进度误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作中各模态的重要性随阶段变化：视觉引导接近，触觉随时间演变决定抓取、对齐与接触。但现有多模态策略通常使用固定的时间上下文与融合策略，忽视了不同技能对每种传感器需求的差异。作者因此研究在基本技能层面如何组合视觉与触觉，即每个技能需要从各传感器获取什么信息。

Method: 提出技能条件化表征：由查询的技能来条件化对模态特定短时观测token的融合，同时注意一个稀疏事件记忆，该记忆保留最近K个已执行技能的终端观测。

Result: 在三个接触密集型任务上以技能进度估计为评价指标：相比微调的SOTA进度模型，滑动检测延迟降低87%；相比仅视觉的消融，扭转完成延迟降低67.5%；借助稀疏事件记忆，盲搜索任务的进度误差降低92%。增益恰好集中在完成由接触或任务历史定义之处。

Conclusion: 结果表明，观测的形成方式（而非仅策略架构）是多模态表征中的核心挑战。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation integrates vision, touch, and language, whose importance shifts across stages: vision guides reaching, while touch, through its evolution over time, decides grasping, alignment, and contact. Yet existing multi-modal manipulation policies typically use fixed temporal contexts and fusion strategies, despite shifts in what each modality contributes across different skills. We study how vision and touch should be combined at the level of primitive skills, asking what each skill needs from each sensor, and propose a skill-conditioned representation in which the queried skill conditions fusion over modality-specific short-term observation tokens while attending to a sparse event memory that retains terminal observations from the last $K$ executed skills. Evaluated by skill progress estimation on three contact-rich tasks, it reduces slip-detection delay by 87% against fine-tuned SOTA progress models, twist-completion delay by 67.5% against a vision-only ablation, and progress error on a blind search task by 92% through sparse event memory. Gains concentrate exactly where completion is defined by contact or task history. More broadly, our results suggest that observation formation not only policy architecture is a central challenge in multi-modal representation. Project Website: http://what-to-attend-what-to-keep.github.io/

</details>


### [19] [Onboard Vision and MPC Navigation for Underwater Robots: An Open BlueROV2 Platform for Multi-Robot Experiments & Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38511)
*Victor Nan Fernandez-Ayala,Wiktor Kowalczyk,Cezary Banaszek,Dimos V. Dimarogonas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个开源BlueROV2平台，集成机载视觉与非线性模型预测控制（NMPC），用于受限环境下的自主导航与对接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主水下机器人在受限环境中作业需要鲁棒的感知、估计与控制能力，而现有平台在集成度、可复现开发和低成本对接支持方面仍有不足。

Method: 平台搭载NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX与Intel RealSense D435i立体相机，封装于模块化耐压舱；采用水下标定立体深度与实时目标检测获取邻近BlueROV2在相机系和机体系下的相对位置；使用基于四元数的估计器融合外部位姿与惯性测量；基于六自由度非线性模型的NMPC控制器跟踪规划好的导航与对接轨迹；并提供开源PX4 SITL、Gazebo仿真环境、多机器人仿真工具及低成本物理对接站。

Result: 实验评估了水下感知、机载计算性能、状态估计、轨迹跟踪与自主对接等多项能力。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个集成感知、估计、控制与开源仿真/物理对接设施的可复现BlueROV2自主导航与对接平台。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater robots require robust perception, estimation and control to operate in confined environments. This paper presents an open-source BlueROV2 platform combining onboard vision with nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) for autonomous navigation and docking. The platform integrates an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX and an Intel RealSense D435i stereo camera in a modular pressure housing. Underwater-calibrated stereo depth and realtime object detection provide relative position measurements of nearby BlueROV2 vehicles in the camera and body frames. A quaternion-based estimator fuses external pose and inertial measurements, while an NMPC controller based on a nonlinear six-degree-of-freedom model tracks planned navigation and docking trajectories. To support reproducible development, we also provide open-source physics-based PX4 SITL and Gazebo environments, multi-robot simulation tools and a lowcost physical docking station. Experiments evaluate underwater perception, onboard computational performance, state estimation, trajectory tracking and autonomous docking.

</details>


### [20] [Behavioral Persistence and Incomplete Functional Transfer of Co-evolved Communication in Evolutionary Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38527)
*Fernando Montes-Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文评估将2D仿真中协同演化出的通信协议直接迁移到3D物理环境（不重新训练网络权重）。两个e-puck机器人（GRU+残差连接）执行觅食任务，迁移仅部分成功且不对称：一个智能体在30个种子中仅1次到达食物，另一个从未到达。加入显式方向信息可改善部分案例，但不足以保证任务成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验在仿真中涌现的通信协议能否免于重训练而直接部署到真实物理机器人上，以探讨涌现通信的现实可迁移性问题。

Method: 两个e-puck型机器人由带残差连接的GRU网络控制，在带社交信号的食物搜寻任务中运行。传感-运动转换层需三项修正以稳定物理运行，其中包括依据训练残差权重中可测量的不对称性来校准"饥饿"项。另设加入显式方向信息的社交通道实验。

Result: 即便经过修正，迁移仍为部分且不对称：30个种子中一个智能体仅1次到达食物源，另一个一次也未能到达（任务成功需两者均抵达）。显式方向信息实验使接收方轨迹出现可观测变化并在若干具体情形下有所改善，但仍不足以让第二个智能体到达食物源。

Conclusion: 局限不单源于信号转换，还来自在新物理约束下的导航能力。涌现通信的成功迁移不仅依赖保留信号过程本身，还依赖保留协议演化时的生态与导航条件。

Abstract: This work evaluates the direct transfer of a co-evolved communication protocol from a 2D simulation to a 3D physical environment, without retraining the network weights. Two e-puck-type robots, controlled by a GRU network with residual connection, were evaluated in a food-seeking task with social signaling. The sensory and motor translation layer required three corrections for stable physical operation, including the calibration of a hunger term based on a measurable asymmetry in the trained residual weights. Even with these corrections, the transfer was partial and asymmetric: one agent reached the food source in one of thirty tested seeds, while the other did not reach it in any. Task success was measured by both agents reaching the food area. An additional experiment incorporating explicit directional information in the social channel produced observable changes in the trajectory of the receiving agent and improvements in several specific cases. However, these improvements were not enough to allow the second agent to reach the food source, suggesting that the limitation may not be explained solely by signal translation, but also by the ability to navigate under the new physical constraints. The results suggest that successful transfer of emergent communication may depend not only on preserving the signaling process itself, but also on preserving the ecological and navigational conditions under which the protocol evolved.

</details>


### [21] [Systematically Exploring the Capabilities of GPT-6 Astra as Embodied Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38537)
*Galbot Team,Xuchuan Chen,Xiaoqian Cheng,Yu Deng,Lihe Ding,Shaocong Dong,Xiangjun Gao,Haozhe Jia,Zekai Li,Zhoujian Li,Yunrui Lian,Sikai Liang,Chenghuai Lin,Dairu Liu,Jiahang Liu,Qingtao Liu,Yuxuan Ma,Zekun Qi,Jiayi Su,He Wang,Ruochen Xu,Tianyu Xu,Xudong Xu,Zhe Xu,Mi Yan,Siming Yan,Li Yi,Ruixi Yu,Jinlu Zhang,Yintianrun Zhang,Zhikai Zhang,Zhizheng Zhang,Yixin Zheng,Weiyi Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 评估GPT-6 Astra作为通用具身策略的能力，发现其在任务决策层面表现有用，但在可靠物理控制上仍存在明显差距，且推理延迟严重制约实际控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPT-6 Astra能够生成数值化机器人动作，其角色已超越高层规划。本文旨在系统评估Astra作为通用具身策略的能力，涵盖直接控制、与学习策略协作以及反馈驱动的自适应三种模式。

Method: 在六个领域开展全面评估：夹爪操作、灵巧操作、移动操作、导航、运动控制和类人全身操作。分别考察直接控制、与学习策略协同（如π0.5、预训练全身控制器）以及基于反馈的自适应表现。

Result: 夹爪操作中可与π0.5混合控制在RoboDojo子集达48%成功；灵巧操作混合控制在十次试炼中达50%成功，但直接手内控制难以协调手指接触；移动操作混合控制在RoboCasa365达38.7%；导航表现较好（RxR指令跟随92%、HM3D物体搜索82%），但搜索路径绕行严重；运动控制中密集运动参考生成不可靠，五次尝试均未达终点；类人操作在30项HumanoidBench任务中有13项超越基线。推理延迟方面，策略辅助与直接控制分别消耗6.248亿与11.32亿tokens，30秒运动任务需250次模型调用、平均每次39.86秒。

Conclusion: Astra在有用任务决策与可靠物理控制之间存在明显鸿沟，训练/推理的高延迟进一步限制其实际部署，表明大模型作为具身策略仍面临控制可靠性与效率的双重挑战。

Abstract: GPT-6 Astra exhibits a remarkable ability to generate numerical robot actions, extending its role beyond high-level planning. To assess Astra's capabilities as general-purpose embodied policies, we conduct comprehensive evaluations across six domains, examining direct control, cooperation with learned policies, and feedback-driven adaptation. In gripper manipulation, Astra can correct task targets and prepare contact conditions for subsequent policy execution; hybrid control with π0.5 achieves 48% success on the evaluated RoboDojo subset. In dexterous manipulation, hybrid control achieves 50% success in ten experience-guided DexJoCo trials, while direct in-hand control struggles to coordinate finger contacts. In mobile manipulation, hybrid control reaches 38.7% success on the evaluated RoboCasa365. In navigation, Astra leads our local comparisons, reaching 92% success on RxR instruction following and 82% on HM3D object search, although search incurs substantial detours. In locomotion, dense motion-reference generation remains unreliable: none of five sequential attempts on a single obstacle course reaches the goal, despite improvements in stability and forward progress. In humanoid loco-manipulation, Astra exceeds baseline methods on 13 of 30 HumanoidBench tasks with pretrained whole-body controllers. These findings reveal a gap between useful task decisions and reliable physical control. Inference latency further constrains practical control: across 50 RoboDojo instances per condition, policy-assisted and direct control consume 624.8 million and 1.132 billion tokens. A 30-second locomotion run requires 250 model calls averaging 39.86 seconds each, with physics paused during inference.

</details>


### [22] [Data-Efficient Adaptation of a Driving VLA to Class 8 Trucks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38570)
*Satyajeet Das,Aaron Buxbaum,Niels Joubert,Gaurav S. Sukhatme*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"adapt-then-steer"策略，将乘用车VLA模型适配到Class 8卡车，用于事故现场和施工区等非结构化场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Class 8卡车在几何、动力学和操控要求上不同于乘用车，为乘用车训练的VLA模型难以直接迁移到卡车，尤其非结构化场景。为避免从零训练，需要一种数据高效的车域迁移方法。

Method: 两阶段：adapt阶段以NVIDIA Alpamayo 1.5为基础，仅微调动作生成模块，使用数百个真实施工与事故相关高速场景；steer阶段提出Flow Velocity Steering (FVS)，一种紧致的、以流时间为条件的残差模块，在每次生成步骤中对动作空间流速度添加学习修正。

Result: 在场景不相交的留出集开环评估中，定向微调使6.4s全时域单候选ADE和FDE较基础模型降低过半；FVS在此基础上进一步将ADE和FDE分别降低13.9%和16.5%。相同数据预算下，定向监督比通用卡车驾驶监督的全时域ADE低19-26%，且与用约65倍通用场景微调的模型相当。

Conclusion: 结果支持adapt-then-steer作为面向Class 8卡车的数据高效车域迁移方案。

Abstract: Class 8 trucks differ from passenger cars in geometry, dynamics, and maneuvering requirements. As a result, vision-language-action (VLA) models trained for passenger vehicles do not readily transfer to Class 8 trucks, particularly in unstructured scenarios such as accident scenes and construction zones. Rather than training a truck-driving VLA from scratch, we propose an adapt-then-steer strategy that adapts an off-the-shelf VLA to generate trajectories for Class-8 trucks in these challenging scenarios. In the adapt stage, we use NVIDIA's Alpamayo 1.5 as the base model, fine-tuning only its action-generation stack on a few hundred real-world construction and accident-related highway scenarios. In the steer stage, we introduce Flow Velocity Steering (FVS) to further refine the model's predictions while holding the adapted VLA fixed. FVS is a compact, flow-time-conditioned residual module that adds learned corrections to the action-space flow velocity used to update the action sequence at each generation step. In open-loop evaluation on a scenario-disjoint held-out set, targeted fine-tuning more than halves single-candidate average displacement error (ADE) and final displacement error (FDE) over the entire 6.4 s horizon compared to the base model. Using the same targeted demonstrations, FVS further reduces the fine-tuned model's full-horizon ADE and FDE by 13.9% and 16.5%, respectively. At matched data budgets, targeted supervision yields 19-26% lower full-horizon ADE than general truck-driving supervision, while the targeted model remains competitive with a model fine-tuned on approximately 65 times as many general truck-driving scenarios. These results support adapt-then-steer for data-efficient vehicle-domain transfer to Class 8 trucks. Our project website is available at https://truckvla.github.io.

</details>


### [23] [Drone Soccer: Learning to Manipulate with Multicopter Downwash](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38588)
*Neelay Joglekar,Bavin Saravanan,Yutong Wang,Varun Kandiyappan,Junyi Geng,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探索利用旋翼下洗气流（downwash）作为主动操作工具，而非干扰，设计无人机足球任务验证可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人空中机械臂（UAM）要么忽略下洗气流，要么将其视为扰动。作者希望主动将下洗气流用作操作工具，解决移动操作任务。

Method: 设计无人机足球任务；建立简化的下洗气流动力学模型；基于该模型用强化学习（RL）训练无人机带球（dribble）策略。

Result: 训练出的RL策略成功迁移到真实世界部署，实现带球操作。

Conclusion: 该工作为多旋翼无人机提供了新型操作能力的关键见解，证明下洗气流可被主动利用完成操作任务。

Abstract: Although multicopter drones are traditionally designed for "perception-only" tasks, like mapping and exploration, recent work has sought to develop Unmanned Aerial Manipulators (UAMs) to solve mobile manipulation tasks. Aerial manipulation performance can be impacted by "downwash," the airflow produced by propellers, but current state-of-the-art UAMs either ignore downwash or treat it as a disturbance. Instead, is it possible to actively use downwash as a tool during manipulation? We design a drone soccer task to explore the feasibility of downwash-based manipulation. Specifically, we develop a simplified downwash dynamics model which we use to train an RL policy to dribble a soccer ball. We further demonstrate that our policy transfers to real world deployment. This work provides key insights into novel manipulation capabilities for multicopters.

</details>


### [24] [FAST-Sync: Fast Group Synchronization for any Matrix Lie Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38594)
*Shane Holmes,Yiran Luo,Firat Taxpulat,David M. Rosen,Frank Dellaert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Fast-Sync是一种针对任意矩阵李群的快速线性近似群同步方法，通过推广弦初始化并利用Kronecker积结构与同步图拓扑来加速，可为局部或全局优化器提供高质量初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群同步(GS)是机器人状态估计和计算机视觉(3D视觉、机器人建图、惯性导航、分子重建)中的核心问题，但通常既高维又非凸，难以求解，因此需要一种能提供高质量初始化的快速方法。

Method: 提出Fast-Sync：一种快速线性近似方法。将弦初始化(chordal initialization)推广到任意矩阵李群，并引入两项算法增强——利用问题数据矩阵中的Kronecker积结构，以及利用同步图的拓扑结构。

Result: 在多个GS任务上的实验表明，Fast-Sync能提供高质量初始化，使局部优化器高效恢复全局最优GS解，即使在较大测量噪声下也能达到高成功率。

Conclusion: Fast-Sync适用于初始化基于局部流形的优化器或可认证的全局方法，兼顾速度、可扩展性与精度，为求解高维非凸群同步问题提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Group synchronization (GS) is the problem of estimating a set of $N$ unknown elements $g_1,\ldots, g_N \in \mathcal{G}$ in a group $\mathcal{G}$, given noisy measurements of a subset of their pairwise ratios $g_i^{-1} g_j$. GS problems lie at the core of many state estimation tasks in robotics and computer vision, including 3D vision, robotic mapping, inertial navigation, and molecular reconstruction. Unfortunately, GS problems are typically both high-dimensional and non-convex, and therefore hard to solve in general. In this paper, we present Fast-Sync, a fast linear approximation method for GS that is suitable for initializing local manifold-based optimizers or certifiable global methods. Our approach generalizes chordal initialization to arbitrary matrix Lie groups, and additionally proposes two new key algorithmic enhancements: we show how to exploit both the Kronecker-product structure in the problem data matrix and the topology of the synchronization graph to improve speed, scalability, and accuracy. Experimental evaluation across several GS tasks demonstrates that Fast-Sync provides high-quality initializations that enable local optimizers to efficiently recover globally optimal GS solutions, achieving high success rates even with considerable measurement noise.

</details>


### [25] [Correcting WHERE, Preserving HOW: Compositional Generalization for Vision-Language-Action Models via Referential Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38616)
*Yanyan Zhang,Disheng Liu,Xinpeng Li,Chaoda Song,Mohsen Hariri,Debargha Ganguly,Wang Yang,Kai Ye,Bryce Grant,Vipin Chaudhary,Yu Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出免训练的Referential Guidance (ReGuide)包装器，通过语义与几何重绑定引导机械臂末端进入演示支持的配置，从而在冻结VLA策略下提升组合泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽能灵活生成动作，但因机器人训练数据缺乏多样性，常利用视觉捷径把动作与任务无关的视觉特征关联，而非任务语义，导致组合泛化（对已见元素的重新组合）受限。现有方法依赖任务相关感知或定向数据多样化，缺乏对未见重组的显式机制，且需针对特定骨干修改并重训练。

Method: 观察到在元素重组下VLA常在全局接地失败、却保留局部操作技能（在熟悉配置中能接近正确目标）。据此提出ReGuide：一种免训练包装器，给定接地模块输出的物体位姿，结合语义与几何重绑定，将末端执行器引导至指令指代物的演示支持配置，使冻结策略可继续执行。

Result: 在仿真中跨多种VLA骨干及真实机器人实验表明，ReGuide在组合偏移下分别将成功率最高提升56.8与75.0个百分点，同时保持标准任务性能。

Conclusion: ReGuide作为免训练、与骨干无关的包装器，有效缓解视觉捷径导致的组合泛化瓶颈，提升分布偏移下的成功率且不损害常规任务表现。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable flexible action generation, their generalization across diverse environmental elements, including manipulated objects, destinations, and backgrounds, is limited by the lack of diversity in robotic training data. Trained end-to-end on such data, VLAs tend to exploit visual shortcuts, associating actions with task-irrelevant visual features rather than the intended task semantics. These shortcuts block recomposition of elements already seen by the policy, that is, compositional generalization. Existing approaches mitigate such entanglement through task-relevant perception or targeted data diversification, but offer no explicit mechanism for unseen recomposition and require backbone-specific modifications with retraining. We observe that under such recomposition, VLAs often fail at global grounding while retaining local manipulation skills that recover near the correct target in familiar configurations. Therefore, we propose Referential Guidance (ReGuide), a training-free wrapper that, given object poses from a grounding module, combines semantic and geometric rebinding to guide the end-effector into demonstration-supported configurations of the instructed referent, where the frozen policy can resume execution. Experiments in simulation across multiple VLA backbones as well as on a real robot show that ReGuide improves success rates under compositional shifts by up to 56.8 and 75.0 percentage points, respectively, while preserving standard-task performance.

</details>


### [26] [Dense Temporal Motion Retargeting for Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38617)
*Jaeryeong Kim,Taerim Yoon,Jin Cheng,Sungjoon Choi,Stelian Coros*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出密集时间运动重定向（DTMR），在单一优化中联合优化时序与控制，采用GPU并行采样MPC，实现对人/动物运动到足式机器人的精确重定向，尤其擅长动态动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源运动（人类/动物）与机器人之间存在形态差异，运动需按机器人动力学特性调整；尤其是跳跃等动态运动，其时序与控制相互依赖，需要精细调整。

Method: 提出DTMR，在同一优化中联合优化时序和控制，且时序在每个控制步都被调整（即"密集"），从而只对需要改变时序的部分进行变形；用GPU上的采样型模型预测控制（MPC）并行求解。

Result: 在两小时人类运动、四种人形机器人上评测，DTMR优于基线，尤其在动态运动上；允许更多时序变形可带来更精确的重定向；与优化时序维度的基线相比，在相同变形预算下重定向更精确且约快19倍；训练得到的策略可迁移到真实人形机器人。

Conclusion: DTMR通过联合且逐控制步的时序-控制优化，实现了精确、高效的运动重定向，并支持真实机器人上的策略迁移。

Abstract: Legged robots can learn expressive whole-body skills from the motions of humans and animals. Due to the morphology gap between the source and the robot, however, the motion must be tailored to the dynamic properties of the robot. In particular, dynamic motions such as a jump require careful adjustment, since their timing and control are interdependent. We propose dense temporal motion retargeting (DTMR), which jointly optimizes timing and control within a single optimization, where dense means that the timing is adjusted for every control step. This dense formulation enables DTMR to deform only the parts of the motion that need a change in timing. The problem is solved with sampling-based model predictive control (MPC) in parallel on a GPU. We evaluate DTMR against baselines on two hours of human motion with four humanoid robots, where the results show that DTMR outperforms baseline methods, particularly on dynamic motions. We also show that allowing more temporal deformation yields more precise retargeting. We further compare DTMR with a baseline that optimizes the temporal dimension, where the result shows that DTMR retargets more precisely under the same deformation budget while being ~19x faster. Lastly, policies trained on our references transfer to a real humanoid robot.

</details>


### [27] [Yggdrasil: a Layer-First 3D Scene Graph for Real-Time Querying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38640)
*Arshia Akhavan,Ermanno Bartoli,Afnan Algharbi,Alireza Hoseinpur,Iolanda Leite,Bryan Donyanavard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Yggdrasil——首个同时面向"生成效率"与"消费效率"设计的 3D 场景图，通过 layer-first 的层级结构统一表达现有流水线的各类表示，并原生支持下游任务的位置与语义查询，查询速度较 DSG 基线最高快 121 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 场景图（3DSG）研究大量聚焦于场景图的生成，却很少关注图在"消费端"（下游任务使用图进行查询）的效率优化。这导致当前先进的感知流水线不得不绕过自己生成的场景图，并付出与感知回路的实时预算不相符的延迟代价。

Method: 提出 Yggdrasil，一种 layer-first 的层级图结构，由通用的节点、边和层（layer）构成。它既能表达已有流水线已经产生的各种表示（室内或室外、扁平或层级），又能原生地回答下游任务发出的位置查询与语义查询。

Result: 与已发表的 DSG 基线相比，Yggdrasil 的查询速度最高快 121 倍；所测量的每个查询耗时在 2 至 127 微秒之间，在工作站与嵌入式设备上均处于 200 微秒关键帧预算内三到五个数量级。将其集成到三个已发表流水线（人体轨迹预测、目标物体导航、人感知运动规划）后，最多消除了各流水线在场景图上 99% 的时间开销。

Conclusion: Yggdrasil 是首个以"生成与消费双重高效"为目标设计的 3D 场景图，能够显著降低下游任务在场景图查询上的延迟，满足实时感知回路的预算要求。实现、基准测试工具及三个集成案例均已在线公开。

Abstract: Robotic agents use 3D scene graphs (3DSG) to perform tasks ranging from scene understanding to scene interaction. Although an extensive body of work addresses scene graph generation, little attention has been paid to optimizing the graph for consumption, which leaves state-of-the-art perception pipelines to work around their own scene graph and to pay a latency cost that does not fit the real-time budget a perception loop runs on.
  We present Yggdrasil, the first 3D scene graph designed to be efficient for both generation and consumption: a layer-first hierarchical graph built from generic nodes, edges, and layers, which expresses the representations existing pipelines already produce, indoor or outdoor, flat or hierarchical, while natively answering the positional and semantic queries downstream tasks issue.
  Against a published DSG baseline, Yggdrasil answers queries up to $121\times$ faster, and every query we measure falls between 2 and 127 microseconds, three to five orders of magnitude inside the 200 microsecond keyframe budget a 3DSG consumer lives in, on both a workstation and embedded class device. We integrate Yggdrasil into three published pipelines spanning human trajectory prediction, object-goal navigation, and human-aware motion planning, where it removes up to 99% of the time each spends on its scene graph. The implementation, benchmark harness, and all three integrations are available online.

</details>


### [28] [TERRA: Terrain-Aware Reconstruction, Retargeting and Control for Musculoskeletal Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38653)
*Merkourios Simos,Chengkun Li,Bianca Ziliotto,Alexander Mathis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TERRA 提出一套端到端流程，从无场景的运动数据中恢复地形几何，并实现地形感知的肌肉驱动运动重定向与控制，使肌骨骼智能体能在多样非平面地形上行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有肌骨骼建模与强化学习虽能让肌肉驱动智能体复现复杂人体动作，但大多局限于平地。原因有二：运动数据集通常缺少与之对齐的地形几何；且将地形交互重定向到复杂肌骨骼身体上十分困难。

Method: TERRA 仅依赖运动学轨迹，融合地形先验、估计的接触信息以及负自由空间证据，恢复任务相关的支撑几何；在重定向过程中引入解剖学约束、肌腱连续性约束与接触约束；基于五个数据集生成运动-地形配对数据。

Result: 在 9.4 小时多样化运动数据上成功训练出单一肌肉驱动控制策略。在重建、重定向与留出跟踪基准上，TERRA 提升了地形精度，显著减少解剖学与交互违规，并在各类支撑地形上取得最高的完成率。

Conclusion: TERRA 为从无场景运动数据到肌肉驱动、跨越多样非平面地形的运动控制提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in musculoskeletal modeling and reinforcement learning have enabled muscle-actuated agents to reproduce increasingly complex human motions. Yet these capabilities remain largely confined to flat ground, in part because motion datasets rarely include aligned terrain geometry and because retargeting terrain interactions to complex musculoskeletal bodies is challenging. We present TERRA, an end-to-end pipeline for terrain-aware retargeting and control of musculoskeletal locomotion. From kinematic trajectories alone, TERRA combines terrain priors, estimated contacts, and negative free-space evidence to recover task-relevant support geometry. TERRA further considers anatomical, tendon-continuity, and contact constraints during retargeting. Using the resulting motion-terrain pairs from five datasets, we successfully train a single muscle-actuated control policy on 9.4 hours of diverse locomotion. Across reconstruction, retargeting, and held-out tracking benchmarks, TERRA improves terrain accuracy, sharply reduces anatomical and interaction violations, and achieves the highest observed completion rate over supported terrain families. Overall, TERRA provides a practical route from scene-less motion data to muscle-actuated locomotion over diverse non-flat terrain. Project website: https://cnai.epfl.ch/terra/

</details>


### [29] [Residual Wrench Certification and Margin-Aware Control Synthesis for Aerial Physical Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38707)
*Abhimanyu Khadga,Abhinav Sinha,Shashi Ranjan Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个任务相对的框架，用于认证有界执行器多旋翼在悬停和接触载荷后的残余力臂权限，并通过凸几何、可计算储备证书、指令投影和增益综合来实现局部跟踪与鲁棒性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多旋翼在执行接触任务（如悬停与接触载荷）时，执行器饱和会限制其残余力臂权限，缺乏任务相对的认证方法，因此需要设计一个可计算、可保证的框架来评估和维持所需储备。

Method: 利用凸几何推导给定凸储备（欧氏球与加权椭球）的有符号裕度；从执行器内部性边界获得可计算储备证书以支持松弛最大化分配；提出指令投影到收紧可行集以保留储备；将可用储备与结构化增益综合相连，并认证尊重执行器限制的局部跟踪区域。

Result: 在局部区域内建立了名义指数收敛与鲁棒最终有界性；对给定任务与形态族，优化八旋翼在等总推力下比优化六旋翼获得更大裕度；闭环仿真验证了固定几何八旋翼和可变倾转四旋翼在持续刚性壁接触中的表现，四旋翼的局部证书对应更高归一化推力极限；超出可实现边界时观察到预测的权限损失（八旋翼受旋翼推力限制，四旋翼受倾转伺服限制）。

Conclusion: 该框架能够任务相对地认证多旋翼在接触载荷后的残余力臂权限，并通过指令投影与增益综合保证局部跟踪性能，同时揭示不同形态在执行器限制下的权限损失机制。

Abstract: We develop a task-relative framework for certifying residual wrench authority after hover and contact loading in multirotors with bounded actuators. Using convex geometry, we derive signed margins for prescribed convex reserves, including Euclidean balls and weighted ellipsoids. We obtain computable reserve certificates from actuator-interiority bounds to support slack-maximizing allocation. To preserve the required reserve, we propose command projection onto a tightened feasible set. We then connect the available reserve to structured gain synthesis and certify a local tracking region that respects actuator limits. Within this region, we establish nominal exponential convergence and robust ultimate boundedness. For the prescribed task and morphology family, we show that the optimized octarotor attains a larger margin than the optimized hexarotor at equal total thrust. We evaluate the proposed framework in closed-loop simulations of sustained rigid-wall contact using a fixed-geometry octarotor and a variable-tilt quadrotor with matched installed thrust. The quadrotor's local certificate admits a higher normalized push limit for the prescribed task family. In tests beyond the realizability boundaries, we observe the predicted loss of authority through rotor-thrust limits for the octarotor and tilt-servo limits for the quadrotor.

</details>


### [30] [CEER2: Directional and Tunable End-Effector and Root Compliance for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38709)
*Xinyuan Luo,Chunyuan Yang,Boyuan Chen,Xianyi Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向人形机器人移动操作任务的柔顺框架，将方向可调的末端执行器柔顺性与可选的本体（root）柔顺性结合，通过分层强化学习控制器实现外力响应与力跟随。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人虽能追踪复杂全身运动，但物理接触带来新挑战：机器人与人或环境接触时，需在应对外力的同时保持任务所需运动，且这种响应在不同方向、不同身体部位上需求各异（如末端执行器某方向顺应接触力、另一方向保持精度；本体可抵抗或跟随外力）。

Method: 提出柔顺框架，结合方向性与可调末端执行器（EE）柔顺，以及可选的本体柔顺（用于外力抑制或力跟随）。采用分层强化学习控制器，通过高层EE与root命令调制固定的全身追踪策略；交互力由本体感受历史估计得出。

Result: 在仿真与真机实验中，人形机器人展示了方向刚度控制、在线刚度调整、不同的本体柔顺行为、柔顺操作以及协同搬运能力。

Conclusion: 该框架可有效协调人形机器人在接触交互中的顺应与运动保持需求，验证了其在复杂移动操作与人机协作任务中的可行性。

Abstract: Humanoids are increasingly capable of tracking complex whole-body motions, but physical interaction introduces a different challenge. When a robot makes contact with a person or the environment, it needs to respond to external forces while preserving the motion needed for the task. This response can vary across directions in the end-effectors and on the body. For example, an end effector may need to accommodate contact force in one direction while maintaining motion accuracy in another, while the robot body may resist an external force or move with it. We present a compliance framework for humanoid loco-manipulation that combines directional and tunable end-effector (EE) compliance with selectable root compliance for external force rejection or force following. A hierarchical reinforcement learning controller modulates a fixed whole-body tracking policy through high-level EE and root commands, while interaction forces are estimated from proprioceptive history. Our simulation and real-world experiments on a humanoid demonstrate directional stiffness control, online stiffness adjustment, distinct root compliance, compliant manipulation, and collaborative carrying.

</details>


### [31] [Path-Following Control and Terramechanics Analysis for Planetary Rovers Under Wheel-to-Wheel Traction Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38715)
*Ryuya Matsuoka,Keisuke Takehana,Kentaro Uno,Toshinori Kuwahara,Kazuya Yoshida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无模型、仅靠减速的滑移转向行星车路径跟随控制策略，通过限制最大指令速度并对弯道外侧车轮选择性减速，使其进入负滑移状态充当机械锚点，从而在持续牵引不对称与可变形松散地形下避免滑沉陷车。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于运动学的控制器在路径修正时加速车轮，容易在松散地形上引发滑移-沉陷并导致车辆被困；滑移转向行星车在持续牵引不对称（如左右履齿高度不同）下会显著偏离路径，亟需一种不依赖模型、避免沉陷的控制方法。

Method: 采用无模型控制策略，仅依靠减速进行路径跟随：设定最大指令速度上限以抑制加速导致的沉陷；航向修正仅通过选择性减速位于转弯外侧的外侧车轮实现，使其进入负滑移区间充当机械锚点。在四轮独立驱动行星车EX1上，以左右履齿高度不同的非对称轮配置进行实验评估，并利用车载多轴传感器进行直接力测量。

Result: 实验表明，该仅减速控制在不同速度工况下（最高0.7 m/s）均能有效抑制累积横向漂移，且不引起沉陷。车载多轴传感器的直接力测量提供了关键地形力学实证：目标减速使底盘载荷实现动态均衡，并完全恢复对侧驱动轮原有的牵引能力。

Conclusion: 仅靠减速的无模型控制可在可变形松散地形和持续牵引不对称条件下实现稳健路径跟随，避免滑沉陷车；其有效性源于对底盘载荷的动态均衡与对侧驱动轮牵引力的恢复。

Abstract: This paper proposes a control strategy for path following that is model-free and relies solely on deceleration for skid-steering planetary rovers navigating deformable loose terrain under continuously imposed traction asymmetry. While conventional controllers that are based on kinematics frequently cause slip-sinkage entrapment by accelerating the wheels during path correction, the proposed approach prevents this failure by setting an upper limit on the maximum commanded velocity. Heading correction is achieved solely through the selective deceleration of the outer wheels, which are located on the outside of the turn, driving them into a negative slip regime to act as a mechanical anchor. The system was evaluated using the four-wheel independent-drive rover EX1 under an asymmetric wheel configuration with different left and right grouser heights that induces significant deviations from the path. Experimental results demonstrate that this deceleration-only control successfully suppresses accumulated lateral drift across various velocity regimes up to 0.7 m/s without causing sinkage. Crucially, direct force measurements from onboard multi-axis sensors provide important empirical evidence of the underlying terramechanics, proving that the targeted deceleration establishes dynamic load equalization across the chassis and completely restores the native thrust capability of the opposite driving wheel.

</details>


### [32] [FlapKAD: A Simulation Dataset of Coupled Wing Kinematics and Aerodynamic Dynamics for Flapping-Wing Aerial Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38719)
*Haichuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FlapKAD——一个包含2000个刚性翼飞行片段、720152个有效时间步的仿真数据集，同步记录双侧翅膀运动学、气动响应与飞行状态，并构建正反双向序列预测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扑翼飞行器实验与建模受限于大规模、时间对齐的数据稀缺：现有数据集规模有限，且受测量噪声及快速变化的翅膀运动与动态响应之间时间错位的影响，尤其在高频扑动时更为严重。

Method: 构建FlapKAD情节式仿真数据集，在同一时钟下同步记录双侧翅膀运动学、气动响应与飞行状态；基于这些时间对齐片段构建统一的双向序列预测基准（正向预测未来垂向力系数与机体垂向速度，反向由响应历史重建未来扑动角与扭转角轨迹）；并用八种代表性时间序列架构在多个预测水平上进行评测。

Result: 基准测试显示：模型行为随预测方向和预测水平而变化；扭转角的重建误差系统性高于扑动角；增加架构复杂度并未带来一致优势。

Conclusion: FlapKAD为研究扑翼飞行器耦合的翅膀运动学、气动与飞行状态动力学提供了可复现的数据集与基准。

Abstract: Experimental investigation and modeling of flapping-wing aerial vehicles are limited by the scarcity of large-scale records that temporally align wing kinematics, aerodynamic responses, and flight states. Existing datasets are often limited in scale and affected by measurement noise and temporal misalignment between rapidly varying wing motion and the associated dynamic response, particularly during high-frequency flapping. We introduce FlapKAD, an episode-structured simulation dataset comprising 2,000 rigid-wing flight episodes and 720,152 valid time steps, with bilateral wing kinematics, aerodynamic responses, and flight states recorded synchronously within a common clock. FlapKAD supports a unified bidirectional sequence-prediction benchmark constructed from the same temporally aligned episodes. The forward task predicts future vertical force coefficients and body vertical velocity from histories of realized flap and twist angles, whereas the inverse task reconstructs future flap- and twist-angle trajectories from the corresponding response histories. A benchmark of eight representative time-series architectures across multiple prediction horizons reveals direction- and horizon-dependent model behavior, systematically higher reconstruction errors for twist angle than for flap angle, and no consistent advantage from increased architectural complexity. FlapKAD provides a reproducible dataset and benchmark for studying coupled wing-kinematic, aerodynamic, and flight-state dynamics in flapping-wing aerial vehicles.

</details>


### [33] [Bounded Channel-Adaptive Spectral Learning for Forward-Consistent Inverse Flapping-Wing Aerodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38730)
*Haichuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BCS-GRU模型，将频谱信息限制为受控的通道自适应残差校正，并引入前向一致性目标，改进扑翼气动反问题的历史感知建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扑翼飞行器通过行程、偏移和俯仰运动的协调变化调节气动力与力矩，产生的载荷既依赖瞬时翼型构型又依赖先前运动历史，因此从期望气动轨迹反推合适的翼运动学是一个具有挑战性的逆问题。现有序列模型能捕捉时间依赖，频谱方法可利用周期性结构，但无约束的频域增强会干扰时间表示，并在各运动学变量和预测时域上产生不一致的修正。

Method: 提出有界通道自适应频谱残差门控循环单元（BCS-GRU），以循环时间预测为主要表示，将频谱信息限制为受控的、针对输出特定的校正。此外引入因果对齐的前向一致性目标，通过单独训练且冻结的气动代理模型评估预测的运动学。

Result: 在统一的回合级协议下，BCS-GRU在逆预测上优于循环基线和自适应频谱基线，且在更长预测时域上收益更大。前向一致微调进一步提升了基于代理的气动一致性，同时保持平均运动学精度。

Conclusion: 受控频谱校正与前向一致性学习为扑翼气动的历史感知逆建模提供了一个有效框架。

Abstract: Flapping-wing vehicles regulate aerodynamic forces and moments through coordinated variations in stroke, deviation, and pitch motion. Because the resulting loads depend on both the instantaneous wing configuration and its preceding motion history, recovering suitable wing kinematics from a desired aerodynamic trajectory is a challenging inverse problem. Existing sequence models capture temporal dependencies, while spectral methods can exploit the periodic structure of flapping motion. However, unrestricted frequency-domain augmentation may interfere with temporal representations and produce inconsistent corrections across kinematic variables and prediction horizons. We propose the Bounded Channel-Adaptive Spectral Residual Gated Recurrent Unit (BCS-GRU), which retains recurrent temporal prediction as its primary representation and restricts spectral information to a controlled output-specific correction. We further introduce a causally aligned forward-consistency objective that evaluates predicted kinematics through a separately trained and frozen aerodynamic surrogate. Experiments under a unified episode-level protocol show that BCS-GRU improves inverse prediction over recurrent and adaptive-spectral baselines, with greater benefits at longer prediction horizons. Forward-consistent fine-tuning further improves surrogate-based aerodynamic consistency while maintaining mean kinematic accuracy. These results demonstrate that controlled spectral correction and forward-consistent learning provide an effective framework for history-aware inverse modelling of flapping-wing aerodynamics.

</details>


### [34] [AIfred: Augmented Learning through Functional Robotic Embodiment at the Desk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38737)
*Gregorio Orlando,Milan Groshev,Eduardo Castelló Ferrer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出AIfred——一台末端执行器搭载投影仪的桌面机械臂，将AI生成的指导直接投射到手写作业旁，实现空间共位的AI辅助，并在用户研究中验证其促进学习迁移与绘画表现的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 桌面学习与创意活动依赖手写参与，但现有生成式AI工具通过独立屏幕提供指导，造成"用户思考之处"与"辅助出现之处"之间的空间割裂。

Method: 设计AIfred：末端带投影仪的桌面机械臂，整合工作区感知、情境感知内容生成与机器人投影；支持数学作业、图像生成和绘画任务。开展n=36的用户研究，与运行在笔记本电脑上的ChatGPT（GPT-5.6 Luna）对照。

Result: 数学任务中辅助可用时两者表现相当（6.7 vs. 7.3/10，p = .41）；撤除辅助后AIfred的短期学习迁移提升60%（7.0 vs. 4.4/10，p = .003）。独立的艺术与设计教授在36例中有33例认为AIfred辅助产出的绘画更优。

Conclusion: 空间共位的AI辅助对那些指导与工作共享同一空间框架的任务更有益。

Abstract: Desk-based learning and creative activities benefit from handwritten engagement. However, current generative AI tools deliver guidance through a separate screen, creating a gap between where users think and where assistance appears. To address this, in this work we design AIfred, a desk-based robotic arm with a projector mounted at the end-effector that places AI-generated guidance alongside handwritten work. AIfred combines workspace perception, context-aware content generation, and robot-mediated projection to support math assignments, image generation, and drawing tasks. In a user study (n = 36), we compared AIfred against ChatGPT (GPT-5.6 Luna) running on a laptop. Both tools performed comparably while assistance was available during the math assignment (6.7 vs. 7.3/10, p = .41), but AIfred improved short-term learning transfer by 60% once assistance was withdrawn (7.0 vs. 4.4/10, p = .003). In addition, independent art and design professors ranked drawings produced with AIfred better in 33 of 36 cases. Our findings indicate that spatially co-located AI assistance benefits tasks whose guidance shares a spatial frame with the work.

</details>


### [35] [HALO: Heterogeneous Allocation Via Localized Observations for the Vehicle Routing Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38760)
*Andrew Meighan,Hyungsub Kim,Or Dantsker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 HALO，一种将车辆路径问题分解为分配与路由两阶段的混合方法，利用异构图神经网络在局部观测下实现实时、去中心化的机器人车队调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车队控制算法多针对集中式、受控环境下的路径规划（最多约1000个任务），未考虑去中心化车队中常见的有限观测与通信范围等现实约束，难以部署到真实场景。

Method: HALO 将 VRP 拆分为分配和路由两部分，为动态环境中的机器人提供在线实时解。分配阶段采用异构图神经网络框架，通过专门的message passing层，将空间分布学习与任务-机器人兼容性学习显式分离。

Result: 在部分可观测的在线 VRP 变体上，HALO 显著优于启发式基线，且解质量与全知离线版本相近；在传统静态单仓库 VRP 上，HALO 相比专为静态变体优化的最先进架构提升最高达14.06%，并在所有测试中保持最快执行时间。

Conclusion: HALO 对观测空间不设严格上界，兼顾部分可观测在线场景与静态 VRP，执行速度最快，具备大规模实时部署潜力。

Abstract: Scalable robotic fleets have become increasingly popular for various applications such as package delivery, warehouse management, and military operations. Prior fleet control algorithms solve centralized routing problems with up to $1{,}000$ tasks in controlled environments, yet they fail to consider realistic constraints such as limited observation and communication ranges typical of decentralized fleets. Thus, deploying existing fleet control algorithms into real-world settings is currently infeasible.
  To tackle this, we propose Heterogeneous Allocation via Localized Observations (HALO) to solve the Vehicle Routing Problem (VRP). HALO is a hybrid method that splits the VRP into allocation and routing portions to provide onboard, real-time solutions to robots in dynamic environments. During the allocation phase, HALO utilizes a heterogeneous graph neural network framework with unique message passing layers to explicitly separate the learning of spatial distributions and task-to-robot compatibility.
  Evaluation results on a partially observable, online variant of the VRP show HALO significantly outperforms the heuristic baseline while maintaining similar solution quality to an all-knowing offline variant of HALO. While HALO is explicitly designed for partially observable environments, it imposes no strict upper bound on the observation space allowing us to test HALO on the traditional static, single-depot VRP. Here, HALO outperforms state-of-the-art architectures strictly optimized for the static variant of the VRP by up to $14.06\%$. Throughout all testing, this framework maintains the quickest execution times which emphasizes its potential for large-scale, real-time deployment.

</details>
