By ensuring that our finite-horizon SNMPC policies decrease the value function in expectation, we can approach the long-horizon performance of the RL approach while satisfying probabilistic safety constraints. Through simulation experiments, we show that our approach can improve the safety of perception-based RL navigation policies and scale to high dimensional systems with large sensor input spaces and complex nonlinear dynamics. We also demonstrate our approach through hardware experiments, showing improved performance for vision-based navigation with an agile fixed-wing aerial vehicle in unknown environments.

</details>


### [105] [Magic-W0: A Structured World-Action Foundation Model for Physical Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39870)
*Xuhua Chen,Zhenhan Yin,Yuan Zhang,Lingfeng Zhang,He Zheng,Tong Mu,Shun Zuo,Dian Zhou,Di Wu,Xuan Zhou,Shaojie Wan,Rongtian Shen,Qiulong Xu,Yiduo Li,Yinglong Wang,Yanqian Wang,Kun Wang,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Magic-W0，一种世界-动作基础模型，通过结构化的“世界状态转移”表征（当前状态、转移、未来状态）联合建模物理状态演化与连续动作，实现预测与控制的紧耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAMs）多依赖未来观测重建或通用隐空间预测，缺乏与动作生成紧密耦合、结构化且面向控制的世界表征，因此难以有效支撑机器人策略学习。作者希望构建更结构化、控制导向的世界表示来弥合预测与动作生成之间的鸿沟。

Method: Magic-W0 将交互表示为结构化的世界状态转移，由三部分组成：(1) 当前状态——视觉-语言上下文结合当前 3D 几何；(2) 转移——以 3D 运动表征动作引起的三维变化；(3) 未来状态——以未来语义描述任务相关结果。提出层对齐的世界-动作交互架构，使不断演化的动作假设条件化地影响世界转移预测，预测的世界表征又持续反馈到动作生成中。在大规模第一视角人类操作、UMI、真实机器人和仿真数据上预训练，并利用预训练视觉模型对几何、3D 运动和未来语义进行隐式监督。

Result: 推理时干预实验表明，结构化世界表征会随候选动作变化而系统性响应，且动作相关信息通过共享的 3D 表征传播到未来语义预测中。在 RoboDojo-Sim 上，Magic-W0 取得平均 Score 27.10，为所比较的 WAMs 中最高；在多个真实机器人任务上，仅用有限下游数据微调后也展现出较强的下游性能，支撑了泛化性与快速适应能力。

Conclusion: 通过结构化的世界状态转移表征与层对齐的世界-动作交互架构，Magic-W0 有效耦合了预测与控制，证明了结构化、控制导向的世界表示在机器人世界-动作基础模型中的价值，并在仿真与真实任务中取得优异表现。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) augment robot policies with action-conditioned environment dynamics, yet existing approaches largely rely on future observation reconstruction or generic latent prediction and lack structured, control-oriented world representations tightly coupled with action generation. We introduce Magic-W0, a world-action foundation model that jointly models structured physical state evolution and continuous actions. Magic-W0 represents interaction as a Structured World Transition consisting of Current State, Transition, and Future State. Current State combines vision-language context with Current 3D Geometry; Transition is represented by 3D Motion capturing action-induced three-dimensional changes; and Future State is represented by Future Semantics describing task-relevant outcomes. To couple prediction and control, we propose a layer-aligned world-action interaction architecture in which evolving action hypotheses condition world-transition prediction, while predicted world representations continuously inform action generation. Magic-W0 is pre-trained on large-scale egocentric human manipulation, UMI, real-robot, and simulation data, with latent supervision for geometry, 3D motion, and future semantics from pre-trained visual models. Inference-time interventions show that structured world representations respond systematically to changes in candidate actions and that action-related information propagates through shared 3D representations into future semantic predictions. On RoboDojo-Sim, Magic-W0 achieves an average Score of 27.10, the highest among the compared WAMs. Across multiple real-robot tasks, it also demonstrates strong downstream performance after fine-tuning with limited downstream data, supporting generalization and rapid adaptation.

</details>


### [106] [SplineWAM: Adaptive Action Horizons for World Action Models via B-Spline Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39873)
*Jun Guo,Xiaoshen Han,Qiwei Li,Nan Sun,Peiyan Li,Heyun Wang,Hang Lai,Weinan Zhang,Xinghang Li,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SplineWAM 用三次B样条参数压缩动作轨迹，实现自适应时间分辨率与异步部署，提升吞吐并降低策略调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）每次推理输出固定长度动作块，计算预算在时间上均匀分配，无法在自由空间运动时执行更久或在接触密集操作上投入更多推理，限制了云端服务的吞吐量。

Method: 将动作轨迹自适应压缩为固定规模的立方B样条参数窗口，节点时间依运动特性拟合；同一参数预算解码出不同时间分辨率与持续时长的动作块，执行跨度与下次调用间隔由预测本身决定。视频监督对齐到演示的拟合节点时间而非均匀网格，使监督帧集中在动作复杂处。异步部署提出 Jacobian-Pullback Real-Time Chunking（JP-RTC），在解码后的原始执行动作上施加块连续性约束，并通过解码器校正参数，使执行前缀与已提交动作一致。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Plus 和 RoboCasa 上，成功率较动作分块 WAM 分别提升8.2与4.4个百分点，每回合策略调用减少22%与26%。在三个异步执行的双臂真实机器人任务上，性能领先或持平基线，每次调用解码执行的运动量提升1.2–1.6倍。

Conclusion: SplineWAM 通过样条参数化动作表示实现了自适应时间分配与高效异步部署，在提升任务成功率的同时显著降低推理调用频率并提高执行吞吐。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) are large embodied policies that jointly predict future video and the actions to execute, emitting a fixed-length action chunk per inference call. Such a policy allocates its computational budget uniformly in time, unable to execute for longer over free-space motion or to spend more inference on contact-rich manipulation, which limits the throughput a WAM can reach when served in the cloud. We present SplineWAM, which adaptively compresses the action trajectory into a fixed-size window of cubic B-spline parameters, fitting the knot times to the characteristics of the motion. One parameter budget then decodes into chunks of varying temporal resolution and duration, and both the executed span and the interval until the next policy call follow from the prediction itself. Aligning the video supervision to the fitted knot times of the demonstration rather than to a uniform grid concentrates the supervised frames where the action trajectory is complex. For asynchronous deployment we introduce Jacobian-Pullback Real-Time Chunking (JP-RTC), which imposes chunk continuity on the decoded raw actions the robot executes rather than on the spline parameters, and corrects the parameters through the decoder so that the executed prefix agrees with the actions already committed. On LIBERO-Plus and RoboCasa, SplineWAM improves success rate over an action chunking WAM by $8.2$ and $4.4$ points while cutting policy calls per episode by 22% and 26%. On three bimanual real-robot tasks under asynchronous execution, it leads or matches the baseline while decoding 1.2 to 1.6 times as much executed motion per call.

</details>


### [107] [Active Mapping of Underwater Litter Using Camera-Sonar Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39898)
*David Rete,Patrick Boros,Lucian Busoniu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种主动建图框架，融合前视声呐与相机共享贝叶斯占据栅格地图，并通过逐步优化选择最佳视角，在水下仿真中比割草机式覆盖和单一传感器更快找到目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋垃圾威胁水下生态系统，需要能在大范围内高效定位碎片自主调查方法。现有方法多沿预设路径或使用单一传感模态：相机在低能见度下图像质量差，声呐噪声大且分辨率低。

Method: 前视声呐与相机共同输入共享贝叶斯占据栅格地图；每步求解优化问题决定下一最佳视角。候选视角由两项效用评分：基于体素熵的探索项与基于可能目标的利用项。各传感器由数据确定随距离和方位变化的检测与虚警概率表。

Result: 在真实感水下仿真中评估，主动建图比割草机覆盖模式更快找到目标，双传感器方法优于任一单独传感器。

Conclusion: 融合声呐与相机的主动建图框架可提升水下垃圾搜索效率，双传感器互补优于单模态方案。

Abstract: Marine litter is a growing threat to the underwater ecosystem, driving demand for autonomous survey methods that can locate debris efficiently over large areas. Existing survey methods typically follow predefined paths or operate with a single sensing modality, typically a camera (with image quality suffering in poor-visibility conditions) or sonar (usually noisy and low-resolution). We present an active mapping framework in which a forward-looking sonar and a camera both feed into a shared Bayesian occupancy map, and an optimization problem is solved at each step to decide on the next best view. Candidate viewpoints are scored by a two-term utility that balances exploration of uncertain regions via voxel entropy against exploitation of likely objects. Each sensor is characterized by range- and bearing-dependent detection and false-alarm probability tables determined from data. We evaluate the approach in a realistic underwater simulator, demonstrating that active mapping finds objects faster than a lawnmower coverage pattern, and that the dual-sensor approach works better than using either of the individual sensors.

</details>


### [108] [Magnetic based In-situ Self 3D Pose Estimation for a Modular Soft Tendon-Driven Continuum Robot via IMU-Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39950)
*Zheng Cao,Guo Ning Sue,Xiangyun Bu,David Quinn,Junzhe Hu,Carmel Majidi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种嵌入式位姿感知框架，结合IMU与主动磁场，在无外部相机条件下估计连续体机器人构型，实现16.7 Hz实时反馈并完成闭环控制验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人因固有柔顺性适合精细操作，但其连续变形结构导致构型估计困难，尤其在外视系统不可用或被遮挡时。因此需要一种不依赖外部相机的嵌入式位姿感知方案。

Method: 融合IMU角度测量与主动磁场参考，提升局部姿态估计并减少运行中累积的姿态误差；构建嵌入式位姿感知框架，实现16.7 Hz更新率的实时反馈。

Result: 通过闭环控制实验验证，利用估计的机器人构型在与物体交互时将末端执行器维持在期望位置，更新率达16.7 Hz。

Conclusion: 分布式磁-惯性感知在连续体机器人的实时位姿估计与闭环控制中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Continuum robots are well suited for gentle manipulation because of their inherent compliance and ability to adapt to complex environments. However, their continuously deformable structure makes accurate configuration estimation challenging, particularly when external vision systems are unavailable or obstructed. In this work, we present an embedded pose sensing framework that combines inertial measurement units (IMUs) and active magnetic fields to estimate the robot configuration without relying on external cameras. The angular measurements from the IMU and magnetic-field references are fused to improve local orientation estimation and reduce accumulated orientation error during operation. This pose sensing scheme achieves an update rate of 16.7~Hz, allowing real-time feedback. The proposed system is experimentally validated through closed-loop control, where the estimated robot configuration is used to maintain the end-effector at a desired position while interacting with an object. These results demonstrate the potential of distributed magnetic--inertial sensing for real-time pose estimation and closed-loop control of continuum robots.

</details>


### [109] [TACTIC: Temporal and Context-Aware LLM Tactical Planning for Roadside LiDAR Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39969)
*Yiming Gao,Shaocheng Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TACTIC 是一个场景感知的路侧 LiDAR 攻击框架，利用多模态大语言模型推断交通语境并自适应配置攻击原语，在 CARLA 中实现 100% 碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理 LiDAR 攻击的效果高度依赖周围交通环境，但现有评估多采用固定原语和人工选参，难以覆盖随场景变化的失效模式。因此需要一个能够感知场景、动态调整战术的协调框架。

Method: 在灰盒威胁模型下，仅依赖攻击方自持的路侧感知栈。本地感知提供车辆度量状态，MLLM 结合路侧图像推断关系型交通语境并构建语义场景图；据此选择并配置 push-away 与 phantom-obstacle braking 两种互补原语，并用实测交通状态与经验校准约束限定在物理可行区间。为应对 MLLM 时延，采用异步重叠推理与执行，由高频本地感知检测场景变化触发重规划。

Result: 280 次随机 CARLA 试验中，完整策略达到 100% 碰撞率，固定规则为 35%、随机选择 60%、受限 LLM（模式选择+默认参数）75%。物理与图像联合输入成功率 100%，仅物理测量 65%，仅图像 75%；异步 Δ 刷新将场景突变响应从 7.4 s 降至 2.0 s。

Conclusion: 依赖场景的战术规划能够暴露固定攻击策略可能遗漏的、对语境敏感的 LiDAR 失效模式，说明交通语境感知与自适应原语配置对物理 LiDAR 攻击评估至关重要。

Abstract: Physical LiDAR attacks are often evaluated using fixed primitives and manually selected parameters, despite their strong dependence on surrounding traffic. We present TACTIC, a scene-aware framework that uses a multimodal large language model (MLLM) to coordinate state-adaptive roadside LiDAR attacks. Under a gray-box threat model, TACTIC relies only on an attacker-operated roadside perception stack, without accessing the victim LiDAR's native point clouds or internal processing. Local perception provides metric vehicle states, while the MLLM combines these measurements with roadside imagery to infer relational traffic context and construct a semantic scene graph. Based on this representation, TACTIC selects and configures two complementary primitives: \emph{push-away}, which shifts the perceived range of a lead vehicle, and \emph{phantom-obstacle braking}, which triggers emergency braking through obstacle injection. Measured traffic states and empirically calibrated constraints ground the generated tactics in physically feasible operating regions. To accommodate MLLM latency, TACTIC overlaps reasoning and execution asynchronously while high-rate local perception detects scene changes and triggers replanning. Across 280 randomized CARLA trials, the full policy achieves a 100% collision rate, compared with 35% for a fixed rule, 60% for random selection, and 75% for a restricted LLM using mode selection with default parameters. Joint physical-and-image input achieves 100% success, versus 65% with physical measurements alone and 75% with imagery alone, while asynchronous $Δ$ refresh reduces scene-mutation response from 7.4 s to 2.0 s. These results show that scene-dependent tactical planning can expose context-sensitive LiDAR failure modes that fixed attack policies may miss.

</details>


### [110] [PhasePlan: Ordered Future-Phase Planning for Robot Brain Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39970)
*Xiaoyu Yang,Yafei Zhang,Wensheng Li,Qing Zhan,Nan Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种有序未来阶段规划方法，通过预测未来动作位置的任务阶段来改善机器人操作的动作时序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人脑模型多预测固定长度的动作块，可能跨越多个任务阶段，从而模糊阶段转换、偏向高频动作模式，损害动态环境中的动作时序。

Method: 从当前多模态观测中预测每个未来动作位置的任务阶段，用所得规划表示来条件化对应动作，保持任务进度与动作生成的时间对齐。训练分两阶段：先学习规划器，再在动作模型微调时冻结规划器以维持稳定的阶段表示。方法在预训练的π_{0.5}和AcrossWAM1.0模型上实例化。

Result: 在传送带操作任务上，相比原始π_{0.5}模型，离线关节动作误差约降低22.5%，并改善了阶段转换建模与跨阶段动作预测。

Conclusion: 有序未来阶段规划对连续动作生成具有价值。

Abstract: Robot brain models integrate vision, language, and robot state to generate actions for complex manipulation tasks. Most predict fixed-length action chunks that may span multiple task phases. This can obscure phase transitions and favor frequent action patterns, compromising action timing in dynamic environments. We propose \method, an ordered future-phase planning method for robot brain models. From current multimodal observations, it predicts the task phase at each future action position. The resulting planning representations condition the corresponding actions, preserving temporal alignment between task progress and action generation. Training first learns the planner, then freezes it during action-model adaptation to maintain stable phase representations. We instantiate \method on pretrained $π_{0.5}$ and AcrossWAM1.0 robot brain models. Detailed quantitative evaluation uses the $π_{0.5}$ implementation. On conveyor-belt manipulation, \method reduces offline joint-action error by approximately 22.5\% relative to the original $π_{0.5}$ model. It also improves phase-transition modeling and cross-phase action prediction. These results demonstrate the value of ordered future-phase planning for continuous action generation.

</details>


### [111] [When Instructions Retrieve Trajectories: Diagnosing and Mitigating Generalization Failures in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39971)
*Hung-Jen Chen,Yu-Hsun Hou,Yan-Hong Chen,Yan-Fu Chen,Binghua Cai,Min Sun,Chun-Yi Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文发现VLA模型存在"指令-动作绑定"失败：模型同时接收语言和视觉输入，却未能将其结合来选择任务所需的动作。作者提出等变反事实训练（ECT），在LIBERO-PRO上将位置交换成功率从36%提升至59%，在真实UR5e上将未见位置成功率从8%提升至88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在分布内任务上成功率可超90%，并能容忍不改变所需动作的干扰变化，但在需要不同动作的反事实变化下却失败。聚合的鲁棒性分数会掩盖这一更具体的失败：策略虽对语言和视觉均有响应，却未将二者结合以选择任务所需的动作，即"指令-动作绑定"失败。

Method: 行为分析显示，失败的 rollout 往往保留源行为或切换到另一已示教任务，说明语言并未被忽略；通过读取与干预将这些选择关联到任务条件下的内部状态。对模仿目标的分析表明，狭窄的条件动作支撑会使基于视觉锚定与基于指令的方案在示教数据上难以区分。据此提出等变反事实训练（ECT），在两个层面发挥作用：数据层面提供同一指令在可区分场景中要求不同动作的有效示教；损失层面在同一更新中将每条示教与其对应样本配对训练。

Result: 在受控的 LIBERO-PRO 对比中，完整 ECT 将 π0.5 的平均位置交换成功率从36%提升至59%。在 CALVIN 上，由于对应样本已存在于原始数据中，ECT 损失无需新示教即可提升五任务完成率。在固定示教预算的真实 UR5e 上，完整 ECT 将未见位置成功率从8%提升至88%。

Conclusion: VLA 模型的失败可归因于指令-动作绑定缺陷，而非单纯的语言忽略。等变反事实训练通过在数据与损失两个层面引入反事实配对，有效缓解该问题，并在仿真与真实机器人上均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models can exceed 90% success on in-distribution tasks and withstand nuisance changes that preserve the required action, yet fail under counterfactual changes that demand a different action. Aggregate robustness scores can therefore conceal a more specific failure, in which a policy responds to both language and vision yet does not combine them to select the action the task requires. We call this failure instruction-action binding. Instructions cue familiar trajectory families, and visual feedback adjusts their execution. Behavioral analyses of fine-tuned $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T-N1.7 policies reveal that failed rollouts often retain the source behavior or switch to another demonstrated task. These switches show that language is not simply ignored. Readouts and interventions connect these choices to task-conditioned internal states. Our analysis of the imitation objective shows how narrow conditional action support can leave grounded and instruction-keyed solutions indistinguishable on the demonstrations. This motivates Equivariant Counterfactual Training (ECT), which acts at two levels. ECT data supply valid demonstrations in which the same instruction requires different actions in distinguishable scenes, while the ECT loss trains each demonstration with its counterpart in the same update. In a controlled LIBERO-PRO comparison, full ECT raises $π_{0.5}$'s mean position-swap success from 36% to 59%. On CALVIN, where counterparts already occur in the original data, the ECT loss improves five-task completion without new demonstrations. On a real UR5e under a fixed demonstration budget, full ECT raises unseen-position success from 8% to 88%.

</details>


### [112] [EWAM: Emergent Depth-Wise Specialization in a Unified Embodied Model -- From Semantic Understanding through Visual Foresight to Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39973)
*Hao Wang,Jiajun Wen,Jingzhi Liu,Shuoshuo Xue,Zhiliang Chen,Min Lin,Yicheng Chang,Xiaoyu Guo,Yukang Zhuo,Zheng Chong,Yunshuang Nie,Jian Zhang,Weijia Liufu,Qingman Wu,Heming Xu,Bingchang Song,Dantong Wu,Zhiyuan Wang,Hang Xu,Jianhua Han,Bokui Chen,Shen Zhao,Rui Li,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EWAM 是一个以动作为中心的统一具身模型，通过非对称联合注意力让动作令牌在每一层读取语义、当前视觉、预测未来与动作信息，并在无层级监督下自发形成“浅层语义—中层未来预测—深层动作”的深度特化；在仿真与真机上超越 VLA、WAM 及混合基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略侧重语义理解，WAM 学习环境动态的预测表征，但现有同时利用两者的系统通常仍把动作计算集中在单一专家上，未能真正融合两类信息。作者希望构建一个统一模型，让动作生成同时受益于语义理解与视觉预见。

Method: 提出 EWAM：以动作为中心的统一具身模型，采用非对称联合注意力，使动作令牌在每层都能读取语义、当前视觉、预测未来帧和动作信息，同时保持各感知专家的独立角色。模型在两种预训练范式下训练：跨本体机器人轨迹与人类第一视角视频。通过 checkpoint 跟踪与因果干预分析内部机制，并引入子任务阶段监督。

Result: 在无层级监督下，EWAM 涌现出深度特化：浅层动作查询主要关注视觉-语言特征，中层关注预测未来帧，深层关注动作令牌本身；该交接跨任务可复现、跨去噪步稳定，且被证明是学习得到的、动作生成所依赖的。仿真与真机实验中，EWAM 超越现有 VLA、WAM 及混合基线；人类第一视角数据提升了跨本体迁移与真机鲁棒性，子任务阶段监督提升了长时程完成率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，统一具身学习能够诱导一种有序的内部进程：从语义理解，经由视觉预见，最终到动作形成。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies emphasize semantic understanding, whereas world-action models (WAMs) learn predictive representations of environment dynamics. Systems that expose a policy to both sources often still concentrate action computation on a single expert. We present EWAM, an action-centric unified embodied model whose asymmetric joint attention lets action tokens read semantic, current-visual, predicted-future, and action information at every layer while the perceptual experts retain their distinct roles. Without layer-wise supervision, EWAM develops an emergent depth-wise specialization: action queries attend mainly to vision-language features in shallow layers, to predicted future frames in intermediate layers, and to action tokens themselves in deep layers. This handoff replicates across tasks and is stable across denoising steps. Checkpoint tracking and causal interventions show that it is learned and that action generation depends on it. EWAM is pretrained in two separate regimes, one on cross-embodiment robot trajectories and one on human egocentric video. In simulation and real-robot experiments, it surpasses existing VLA, WAM, and hybrid baselines. Human egocentric data improve both cross-embodiment transfer and real-robot robustness, and subtask-phase supervision improves long-horizon completion. Together, these results suggest that unified embodied learning can induce an ordered internal progression from semantic understanding, through visual foresight, to action formation.

</details>


### [113] [Multi-Link Safety Filtering for VLA Policies Around Moving Hazards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40007)
*Yatharth Agarwal,Vijay Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种免训练的运行时安全屏蔽方法，用五个椭球覆盖夹爪、手腕和前臂，并结合RGB-D初始感知与稀疏光流跟踪危险物，使预训练VLA策略在杂乱环境中避免碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略即使成功完成任务，也可能撞倒无关物体，仅凭任务成功率无法证明其在杂乱环境中部署的安全性；且需要在不重训模型的前提下，在运行时保护整条手臂并跟随移动的危险物，同时与策略共享机载算力。

Method: 免训练安全屏蔽：用五个椭球覆盖夹爪、手腕和前臂；在复位时由RGB-D感知拟合一个禁入椭球，并通过单一障碍函数程序过滤每条运动指令；随后用稀疏光流携带该椭球中心随危险物移动，无需重复检测或重新拟合。同时在边缘硬件上裁剪视觉-语言前缀并减少流匹配步数。

Result: 六种仿真危险物运动条件下，碰撞率从65.62%降至27.27%，安全成功率从29.35%升至50.43%；消融表明保护手臂连杆优于仅保护末端，跟踪可挽回冻结估计下的大部分防护。五椭球障碍在CPU上99百分位耗时2.2ms，π0.5策略调用在集成GPU上从343ms缩短至177.3ms。在SO-101实体机械臂四个任务中，屏蔽后16次中3次触碰危险物，未屏蔽为16次中11次。

Conclusion: 该免训练屏蔽方法可在不重训的前提下显著降低VLA策略的碰撞风险，兼顾手臂多连杆保护与移动危险物跟踪，并可在异构边缘硬件上实时运行，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: A vision-language-action (VLA) policy can finish a manipulation task while knocking over objects unrelated to it, so task success alone does not show that the policy is safe to deploy in clutter. We study how to keep a pretrained VLA policy clear of such hazards at run time without retraining it, which requires guarding more of the arm than the end effector, following the hazard as it moves, and sharing onboard compute with the policy. Our training-free shield covers the gripper, wrist, and forearm with five ellipsoids and filters every commanded motion through one barrier program against a keep-out ellipsoid fitted from RGB-D perception at reset. Sparse optical flow then carries that ellipsoid's center along with the hazard, with no repeated detection or refitting. Over six simulated hazard-motion conditions, the shield lowers collision from $65.62\%$ to $27.27\%$ and raises safe-success, task completion without collision, from $29.35\%$ to $50.43\%$. Ablations show that guarding the arm links protects beyond end-effector shielding, and that tracking recovers most of the protection lost when the hazard estimate is frozen at reset. On heterogeneous edge hardware, the five-ellipsoid barrier runs on the CPU in $2.2$~ms at the 99th percentile, and trimming the vision--language prefix and taking fewer flow-matching steps shortens each $π_{0.5}$ policy call on the integrated GPU from $343$ to $177.3$~ms. On a physical SO-101 arm across four tasks, the arm touched the hazard in 3 of 16 shielded episodes versus 11 of 16 unshielded ones. Project page: https://yathag.github.io/multilink-safety-filter/

</details>


### [114] [Identifiable Decomposition of Submovements in Human Hand Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40012)
*Adrian Prados,James Hermus,Ramon Barber,Sylvain Calinon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出可识别性判据（时空核相关）并构建 Sub-ID 方法，实现人体运动基元的可识别分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有次运动（submovement）分解方法在大规模数据上扩展性差，且缺乏判断分解是否有信息量的原则性方法。

Method: 以基元对之间的时空核相关作为可识别性判据，并将其嵌入自适应岭正则化中，使优化偏向低相关解；当基元共线时可识别性丧失，空间分离时恢复。

Result: 合成数据上，Sub-ID 能恢复真值参数分布（优于现有方法），且在基元重叠难以区分时显式检测歧义而非输出误导结果；并在真实三维长时程运动中成功提取次运动，为现有方法未覆盖的场景。

Conclusion: Sub-ID 有望为运动控制研究与模仿学习识别具有生理基础的基元。

Abstract: Voluntary movements have long been hypothesised to be comprised of discrete primitives called submovements, as a descriptive model of human motor behaviour. However, existing methods scale poorly, and no principled method exists to determine whether a decomposition is informative. We propose a spatiotemporal kernel correlation between primitive pairs as an identifiability criterion. Submovement-Identifiable Decomposition (Sub-ID) embeds this criterion in its adaptive-ridge regularisation, biasing the optimiser toward low-correlation solutions. Identifiability is lost when primitives become collinear and recovered when they diverge spatially. On synthetic data, Sub-ID recovers ground-truth parameter distributions where existing methods fail; furthermore, when primitives overlap too heavily to be distinguished, the method explicitly detects this ambiguity rather than outputting misleading results. Sub-ID extracts submovements from real three-dimensional, long-horizon movements, a regime no prior method addresses. This method has the potential to identify physiologically grounded primitives for motor control research and imitation learning.

</details>


### [115] [BatSLAM 2.0: Sequence-Verified Sonar Place Recognition in a Robust Pose Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40085)
*Jan Steckel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出BatSLAM 2.0，一种仅依赖声呐的SLAM系统，通过改进的声学前端、序列验证器和高性能因子图位姿图，解决了声呐地点识别歧义导致的错误闭环和地图崩溃问题，并在仿真与真实数据中验证了鲁棒的拓扑建图能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期BatSLAM证明了仿生双耳声呐机器人可构建拓扑地图，但声呐地点识别存在天然歧义：走廊会产生几乎相同的回波序列，错误的闭环检测会导致拓扑地图崩溃。因此需要一种能应对歧义、防止地图崩溃的鲁棒声呐SLAM方法。

Method: BatSLAM 2.0由三部分组成：(1) 更新的声学前端；(2) 序列验证器，用于跟踪并验证闭环候选；(3) 在高性能因子图框架上实现的位姿图。

Result: 系统在仿真环境和真实世界录音中均进行了充分评估，结果表明BatSLAM 2.0能够鲁棒地创建拓扑地图、抵抗地图崩溃，并能稳健地扩展地图规模。

Conclusion: BatSLAM 2.0通过序列验证与因子图优化，有效缓解了声呐地点识别的歧义问题，实现了鲁棒且可扩展的仅声呐SLAM建图。

Abstract: Echolocating bats can navigate dark and cluttered spaces using echolocation. Over a decade ago, BatSLAM showed that a robot with a biomimetic binaural sonar can build a topological map of the environment, by recognizing places from the received acoustic signals. Sonar place recognition, however, is ambiguous by nature: corridors produce nearly identical echo trains, and wrong loop closure can collapse the topological map. In this paper, we introduce BatSLAM 2.0, a novel sonar-only SLAM system built from three elements: an updated acoustic front-end, a sequence verifier that tracks and verifies loop closure candidates and a pose graph implemented on a high performance factor graph framework. The system was thoroughly evaluated both in simulated as well as real world recordings. In both cases, the BatSLAM2.0 algorithm shows the capability of robust topological map creation, countering map collapse, and robust scaling of map size.

</details>


### [116] [Passive Stiffness Shaping in Cable-Suspended Aerial Manipulation via Movable Compliant Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40102)
*Antonio Franchi,Amr Afifi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将多无人机视为可移动柔性锚点，建立重力感知的准静态理论，用于预测并塑造缆索悬挂负载的被动笛卡尔刚度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缆索悬挂空中操作虽然具有轻量、适合协同运输与物理交互的优势，但负载处感受到的被动机械响应尚未被充分理解和系统利用，因此需要建立可预测、可调控的刚度理论。

Method: 针对任意数量无人机通过绷直、直线、不可伸长缆索连接点负载的情形，在选定重力加载平衡点下，将每个支腿中的空中锚点柔顺性与缆索横向几何柔顺性视为串联，各支腿刚度在负载处并联；在锚点各向同性条件下，每个支腿等价于虚拟单侧弹性缆索，并给出由平衡张力主导的轴向—横向刚度分解，进而构建从指令锚点构型到被动负载刚度的非线性映射及其微分形式的局部保约束塑形方法。

Result: 推导出支腿等价虚拟单侧弹性缆索及轴向—横向刚度分解，建立了锚点构型到负载刚度的非线性映射，并说明可通过锚点重定位实现局部约束保持的刚度塑形；同时提出包含非线性车辆控制、弹性阻尼腱和环境接触的动态刚体验证框架，用于评估该刚度预测在超出解析假设时的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为缆索悬挂空中操作中负载被动刚度提供了一种重力感知的准静态预测与塑形理论，并给出动态验证框架以检验其适用边界与预测能力。

Abstract: Cable-suspended aerial manipulation offers a lightweight architecture for cooperative transportation and physical interaction, yet the passive mechanical response perceived at the load remains insufficiently understood and systematically exploited. This work interprets aerial vehicles as movable compliant anchors and develops a gravity-aware quasi-static theory for predicting and shaping the passive Cartesian stiffness of a suspended load. The formulation applies to an arbitrary number of aerial vehicles connected to a point load by taut, straight, inextensible cables. At a selected gravity-loaded equilibrium, aerial-anchor compliance and transverse cable geometric compliance combine in series within each leg, while the leg stiffnesses act in parallel on the load. For isotropic aerial-anchor behavior, each leg is exactly equivalent to a virtual unilateral elastic cable, revealing an axial--transverse stiffness decomposition governed by the equilibrium tension. These results define a nonlinear map from commanded-anchor configuration to passive load stiffness, whose differential enables local constraint-preserving shaping through anchor repositioning. A dynamic rigid-body validation framework with nonlinear vehicle control, elastic-damped tendons, and environmental contact is defined to assess when and to what extent the derived stiffness remains predictive beyond the assumptions of the analytical model.

</details>


### [117] [Tactile Curiosity Drives Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40134)
*Klemens Iten,Alexander Proshkin,Bhavya Sukhija,Stelian Coros,Andreas Krause,Pieter Abbeel,Carmelo Sferrazza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacEx 将触觉反馈引入基于认知不确定性的探索，把好奇心导向触觉通道，从而在无任务奖励与专家示范条件下学习接触丰富的操作技能，并支持离线策略学习与 VLA 后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在机器人操作中样本效率低：常用算法依赖随机动作采样，训练预算大量消耗在远离接触的无意义自由空间运动上；已有的基于模型分歧或认知不确定性的内在动机方法虽优于各向同性噪声，但会奖励功能无关的过渡（如自由空间中的乱动）中的不确定性。

Method: 本文认为触觉反馈是探索的自然信号，提出 TacEx 框架：将模型不确定性按感知模态分解，把好奇心定向到触觉通道，以触觉驱动的好奇心引导机器人发现复杂接触动力学。探索过程无需任务奖励或专家示范，收集交互密集的数据集用于下游离线学习，并用于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的后训练。

Result: 触觉驱动的好奇心使机器人自主发现复杂接触动力学，学会操作与抓取物体；所收集的交互密集数据集支持在无额外环境交互情况下离线学习下游取放（pick-and-place）策略；用 TacEx 对最初未使用触觉预训练的 VLA 模型进行后训练，显著提升下游性能且保持高样本效率。

Conclusion: 将认知不确定性探索锚定于触觉模态是可行且高效的方向，可替代任务奖励与专家示范，为接触密集型操作技能的自主获取、离线策略学习以及 VLA 模型的后训练提供统一且高样本效率的方案。

Abstract: Mastering robot manipulation skills via reinforcement learning (RL) remains largely sample-inefficient. The most common RL algorithms rely on random action sampling to discover new strategies, resulting in agents that allocate most of their training budget to motions in free space, away from the contacts from which manipulation skills emerge. Existing intrinsic motivation methods based on model disagreement or epistemic uncertainty improve on isotropic noise, but they can also reward uncertainty in functionally irrelevant transitions, such as erratic motions in free space. In this work, we argue that tactile feedback provides a natural signal for exploration, and introduce TacEx, a framework that incorporates touch into epistemic uncertainty-driven exploration by decomposing model uncertainty across sensory modalities and directing curiosity toward the tactile channel. By anchoring curiosity to the sense of touch, TacEx drives the robot to discover complex contact dynamics, learning to manipulate and grasp objects without task rewards or expert demonstrations during exploration. The interaction-dense dataset collected through this tactile-driven curiosity supports offline learning of downstream pick-and-place policies without additional environment interaction. We further use tactile-driven exploration to post-train vision-language-action (VLA) models. Although the VLAs are initially pre-trained without tactile feedback, post-training with TacEx substantially improves downstream performance while remaining highly sample-efficient.

</details>


### [118] [Dream4ACT: A Shared Visual Action Interface for Multi-Embodiment Video-Action Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40153)
*Xiangyu Zhu,Jin Xu,Yue Guo,Xin Wu,Yifan Sun,Xiancong Ren,Jianxin Sun,Yong Dai,Xiaozhu Ju*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Dream4ACT提出一种跨具身的世界模型，通过"动作视图"这一共享视觉动作接口统一不同机器人的动作表示，结合掩码流匹配实现视频-动作联合建模，并在RoboTwin 2.0和TriWorldBench上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型蕴含丰富的时空先验，可用于具身观测-动作建模。但关节空间动作向量缺乏显式的图像空间结构，且在不同具身之间维度与语义各异，难以直接利用视频生成模型的时空先验。末端执行器可视化虽为替代方案，却无法完整描述机器人执行所需的全部关节构型。

Method: 提出Dream4ACT世界模型用于跨具身关节视频-动作建模。引入共享视觉动作接口——动作视图（action views），基于URDF前向运动学，从四个预设虚拟相机渲染目标关节构型，在保留具身特定关节几何的同时让观测与动作序列共享视频自编码器和扩散Transformer。通过掩码流匹配，单一联合训练模型即可支持前向动力学、逆向动力学和联合观测-动作生成。并提出免训练、受URDF约束的多视图恢复机制，从预测的动作视图中还原可执行动作序列，无需学习具身特定解码器。

Result: Dream4ACT在RoboTwin 2.0上取得平均88.98%的成功率，在TriWorldBench上获得65.66的总分，支持有效的闭环操作，并通过视觉动作接口实现具有竞争力的动作条件多视图预测。

Conclusion: 通过共享视觉动作接口与掩码流匹配，Dream4ACT有效统一了跨具身的视频-动作表示，验证了利用视频生成模型先验进行具身操控的可行性，为跨具身世界模型提供了新思路。

Abstract: Video generation models (VGMs) offer strong spatiotemporal priors for embodied observation--action modeling. However, joint-space action vectors lack explicit image-space structure and vary in dimensionality and semantics across embodiments, making it challenging to directly leverage the rich spatiotemporal priors of VGMs. End-effector visualizations provide an alternative but do not specify the full articulated configuration needed for robot execution. We present Dream4ACT, a world model built for joint video-action modeling across embodiments. To unify action representations across embodiments, we introduce a shared visual action interface, called action views, which render target joint configurations from four prescribed virtual cameras using URDF-based forward kinematics. This shared visual representation preserves embodiment-specific articulated geometry while allowing observation and action sequences to share a video autoencoder and diffusion transformer. Through masked flow-matching, our model supports forward dynamics, inverse dynamics, and joint observation--action generation within a single jointly trained model by varying which future sequences are corrupted. To recover executable action sequences from predicted action views, we propose a training-free, URDF-constrained multiview recovery mechanism, without a learned embodiment-specific decoder. Dream4ACT achieves an average success rate of 88.98\% on RoboTwin~2.0 and an overall score of 65.66 on TriWorldBench, supporting effective closed-loop manipulation and competitive action-conditioned multiview prediction through the visual action interface.

</details>


### [119] [Rethinking Legibility in Social Robot Hallway Navigation: Impact of Intent Representation and Human Distraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40158)
*Pranav Goyal,Andrew Stratton,Christoforos Mavrogiannis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究动态行人环境下社交导航中机器人可读性运动生成，通过两项用户实验考察意图表示方式与行人注意力水平对导航表现及人类主观感受的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可读性运动生成框架多针对静态观察者，而社交机器人导航面临新挑战：机器人在行人注意力分散的动态环境中，既要清晰传达意图又要保证人类安全。因此作者希望探究表示方式与人类注意力水平如何影响动态受限空间中的导航表现与人类印象。

Method: 聚焦走廊导航场景，在共享的模型预测控制（MPC）框架下实现多种可读性表述。研究一（N=45）考察意图表示的作用，对比基于目的地、基于通过侧（含自适应更新）及不可读基线；研究二（N=45）考察行人注意力分散的影响。

Result: 研究一表明，通过侧可读性（尤其是自适应更新时）使人类运动更平滑，并被感知为更具能力、心理与身体负担更低，优于基于目的地和不可读基线。研究二表明，可读性运动即使在行人分心时也能带来平滑的人类运动，尽管这一点未一致反映在主观评分中。

Conclusion: 社交机器人导航中有效的可读性运动受益于交互层面的意图表示以支持协调，且部分效果在人类注意力分散时仍能保持。

Abstract: We focus on legible robot motion generation in social navigation settings. Legibility in human-robot interaction (HRI) is often described as the property of robot motion that enables an observer to confidently infer the robot's intent. While mature frameworks exist for generating legible motion in front of static observers, social robot navigation presents a new challenge: the robot must clearly convey its intent while ensuring human safety in dynamic pedestrian environments where human attention is often divided. With the goal of enabling robots to generate legible motion in dynamic and constrained spaces, we investigate how the choice of representation and the level of human attention shape navigation performance and human impressions. Focusing on the ubiquitous and demanding scenario of hallway navigation, we conduct two controlled user studies involving alternative legibility formulations implemented within a shared model predictive control framework. Study 1 (N = 45) investigates the role of intent representation, showing that passing-side legibility, particularly when adaptively updated, leads to smoother human motion and is perceived as more competent and less mentally and physically demanding than destination-based and non-legible baselines. Study 2 (N = 45) examines the effect of pedestrian attention, demonstrating that legible motion allows for smooth human motion even under distraction, even if this is not consistently reflected in subjective ratings. Together, these findings suggest that effective legible motion in social robot navigation benefits from interaction-level intent representations that support coordination, with some effects persisting even when human attention is divided. Code is available at https://github.com/fluentrobotics/Legible_MPPI.

</details>


### [120] [PrefPI: Preference-Guided Steering into Out-of-Distribution Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40165)
*Seungeun Rho,Wontaek Kim,Danfei Xu,Sehoon Ha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PrefPI 提出一种基于相对偏好引导的迭代框架，通过偏好条件生成建模与 CFG，实现对预训练生成式机器人策略的超越初始支撑范围的引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好学习方法主要强化策略已具备的行为模式（模态），难以引导到初始策略极少或从未展现的目标行为。论文希望解决"超越初始有效支撑范围"的策略引导问题。

Method: 将偏好学习形式化为偏好条件生成建模：偏好轨迹定义条件分布，其与更广行为先验的密度比提供隐式偏好信号，并用无分类器引导（CFG）放大该信号；重复偏好条件建模与引导步骤，形成偏好引导的策略迭代。

Result: 在扩散策略与 PI0.5 flow-matching VLA 上，仿真与真实环境中均以有限反馈实现显著行为转变；真实硬件上仅用 150 条偏好标注轨迹，将物体搬运高度从 10.7 cm 提升至 19.8 cm。

Conclusion: 偏好条件生成建模结合 CFG 能有效引导预训练生成式策略超越初始行为分布，实现样本高效的行为跃迁。

Abstract: We present PrefPI (Preference-Guided Policy Iteration), an iterative framework for steering pretrained generative robot policies using only relative preferences over self-generated trajectories. Unlike prior preference-learning methods that primarily sharpen modes already represented by the policy, we study steering beyond the initial effective support, where desired behaviors are rarely or never observed under the initial policy. Our key idea is to formulate preference learning as preference-conditioned generative modeling: preferred trajectories define a conditional distribution, whose density ratio with the broader behavior prior provides an implicit preference signal amplified by classifier-free guidance (CFG). Repeating this preference-conditioned modeling and guidance step yields a form of preference-guided policy iteration, turning incremental improvements toward previously inaccessible behaviors. Across diffusion policies and the PI0.5 flow- matching VLA in simulation and the real world, PrefPI produces substantial behavioral shifts with limited feedback. In particular, PrefPI increases object transport height from 10.7 cm to 19.8 cm on real hardware with only 150 preference-labeled trajectories.

</details>


### [121] [Social-WM: Safety-Aware Latent World Models for Robot Social Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40177)
*Zhihao Zheng,Mooi Choo Chuah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Social-WM，一种从自我中心 RGB 视频训练的潜在世界模型规划框架，通过"名义动作-可执行动作"差异为社交导航提供安全性信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交导航要求机器人不仅预测动作的未来后果，还要判断名义动作在周围物理与社会约束下是否真正可执行。

Method: 从自我中心 RGB 视频训练潜在世界模型；以动作条件化未来预测学习与安全相关的后果（目标为命令后实际观测到的未来）；引入可执行逆动力学目标，将观测到的潜在转移与实际实现的动作而非名义动作关联；部署时通过潜在世界模型想象候选动作，用逆动力学模型估计其可执行性，以名义-可执行差异作为执行前的安全信号。

Result: 在 Social-HM3D 上成功率 63.77%，人类碰撞降至 21.67%；在零样本迁移到 Social-MP3D 时保持较强性能，且无需显式行人跟踪、特权人体状态或在线强化学习。

Conclusion: 学习的动力学与可执行性模型保持目标无关，同时支持位置目标和图像目标导航；名义-可执行差异可作为有效的预执行安全信号。

Abstract: Safe social navigation requires a robot to anticipate not only the future consequences of its actions, but also whether a nominal action can actually be executed under surrounding physical and social constraints. We present Social-WM, an efficient latent world-model planning framework trained from egocentric RGB video sequences. Our key observation is that social-navigation experience contains a systematic discrepancy between the nominal action and the realizable action: a nominal forward action may be fully executed in free space, but needs to be constrained when heading towards a pedestrian or obstacle. Social-WM learns these safety-relevant consequences directly through action-conditioned future prediction, where the target is the actual observed future following each command. We further introduce a realizable inverse-dynamics objective that associates observed latent transitions with the action actually realized rather than the nominal one. At deployment, candidate actions are imagined through the latent world model, and the inverse dynamics model estimates their realizability; nominal--realizable discrepancy then provides a safety signal before execution. The learned dynamics and realizability model remain goal-independent and support both position- and image-goal navigation. On Social-HM3D, Social-WM achieves 63.77% success while reducing human collisions to 21.67%, and maintains strong performance under zero-shot transfer to Social-MP3D, without explicit pedestrian tracking, privileged human state, or online reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [122] [Non-Invasive Inspection of Water Canals Using Dronar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40178)
*Michael Zielinski,Zhizhan Wang,Benjamin Dymond,Reza Razavian,Zhongwang Dou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究团队提出并验证了"dronar"系统——将消费级声呐集成于无人船（USV）上、贴水面探测，实现无需排水的混凝土渠道渠床无损检测。在凤凰城亚利桑那渠三次现场测试中，泥沙段深度剖面比清洗后浅至多15 cm，重复测量结果高度重合，验证了该方案的可重复性与可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放的混凝土渠承担着亚利桑那州凤凰城地区数百万人的输水任务，长期运行会出现衬砌变形、混凝土开裂和渠底泥沙淤积等问题。目前仅在四年一次的断水周期内才能检查，过程耗费大量资源，导致维护人员无法优先处理受损最严重的渠段。因此需要一种易于部署、无需排水的无损检测方法。

Method: 构建"dronar"检测系统：采用低成本、货架可得的消费级声呐，集成到无人水面艇（USV）上，使声呐换能器保持在渠道水面下方。在凤凰城亚利桑那渠开展三次现场试验，采集DownScan深度剖面，对比含泥沙渠段与清洗后同一渠段的剖面，并进行清洁段重复航行测试以评估可重复性。

Result: 泥沙淤积渠段的DownScan深度剖面一致地浅于同一渠段清洗后的剖面，沿测线偏移最高达15 cm。在清洁渠段上重复运行的深度剖面高度重合，说明dronar在渠床自然变化条件下仍能给出可重复的测量结果。

Conclusion: 结果表明dronar是一种可行、无需排水即可进行渠床检测的概念验证工具，为混凝土渠道的低成本、易部署、无损巡检提供了新途径。

Abstract: Open concrete canals play a vital role in water transportation, serving as primary water infrastructure for millions of people across the Phoenix, Arizona, metro area. Over time, the concrete canals can experience a range of issues, including canal lining deformation, cracked concrete, and sediment buildup on the canal floor. Identifying such critical issues is a resource-intensive process, which currently happens only during four-year dry-up cycles. This prevents the maintenance crew from prioritizing operations on the most affected canal segments. To address this issue, the research team has developed and verified an easily deployable and non-invasive method to inspect canal beds without draining the water. This inspection system integrates affordable, off-the-shelf drone and sonar technology (termed dronar). This dronar system includes a consumer-grade sonar system integrated into an unmanned surface vehicle (USV) that carries the sonar transducer just under the surface of the canal water. This paper presents a proof-of-concept demonstration of the dronar system across three field tests on the Arizona Canal in Phoenix. DownScan depth profiles from the sedimented canal segment were consistently shallower than profiles from the same segment after cleaning, with offsets of up to 15 cm observed along the track. Repeated runs over the clean segment produced closely overlapping DownScan depth profiles, confirming that the dronar yields repeatable measurements across the natural variation of the canal bed. These results establish the dronar as a viable proof-of-concept tool for non-invasive canal bed inspection.

</details>


### [123] [Centralized Multi-UAV Exploration and 3D Reconstruction Using Single-UAV Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40208)
*João Félix Mendes,Meysam Basiri,Rodrigo Ventura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个集中式多无人机探索框架，使现有单机采样规划器可直接用于多机场景，实验表明分散部署(SS)比集中部署(JS)探索更快、覆盖更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将单无人机探索方法扩展到多无人机团队可提升覆盖速度与鲁棒性，但会带来一致建图、安全导航和部署策略等挑战，现有单机采样规划器难以直接应用。

Method: 构建集中式多无人机探索架构，多机共享全局TSDF地图并集中规划；基于voxblox改造建图流程，实现多机深度测量实时融合到统一TSDF；集成机间避障与机器人自过滤机制，避免将其他无人机重建为静态障碍。

Result: 在仿真中用四种采样规划器(RH-NBVP、KRH-NBVP、AEP、KAEP)进行评测，保留其核心采样逻辑，仅做系统级适配；在多种环境与两种部署配置(JS集中启动、SS分散启动)下测试，结果显示SS在所有规划器中均实现更快的探索速度与更好的覆盖效果。

Conclusion: 该框架可在多机场景下复用现有单机采样规划器，并表明部署策略对多无人机探索性能具有重要影响。

Abstract: Extending single Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) exploration methods to multi-UAV teams can improve coverage speed and robustness, but introduces challenges such as consistent mapping, safe navigation, and deployment strategy. In this work, we present a centralized multi-UAV exploration framework that enables the use of existing single-UAV sampling-based planners in a multi-UAV setting.
  The proposed architecture allows multiple UAVs to collaboratively explore unknown environments using a shared global Truncated Signed Distance Field (TSDF) map and centralized planning. Building on the voxblox library, we adapt its mapping pipeline to support real-time fusion of depth measurements from multiple UAVs into a common TSDF representation. In addition, inter-UAV collision avoidance and robot self-filtering mechanisms are integrated into the system to ensure safe navigation and prevent reconstruction of other UAVs as static obstacles.
  The framework is evaluated in simulation using four sampling-based exploration planners - RH-NBVP, KRH-NBVP, AEP, and KAEP - whose core sampling logic is preserved, with only system-level adaptations for multi-UAV operation. Experiments are conducted across multiple environments and under two deployment configurations: Joint Start (JS), where UAVs are initialized in close proximity, and Separated Start (SS), where UAVs are initialized in distinct locations. Results show that SS deployments consistently achieve faster exploration and improved coverage across all planners, highlighting the importance of the deployment strategy in multi-UAV exploration performance.

</details>


### [124] [STARS: From Spatiotemporal Dynamics to Social Representations in Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40245)
*Nathan Tsoi,Michael J. Munje,Tejas Oberoi,Rishab Maheshwari,Pengen Zheng,Tanush Chauhan,Peter Stone,Joydeep Biswas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 SocialNav-SUB 基准，用 VQA 任务评估 VLM 在真实社交机器人导航场景中的场景理解能力，发现当前 VLM 虽接近人类答案，但仍不及简单规则基线和人类共识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人导航需要基于稳健场景理解的社会化决策，VLM 虽有潜力，但尚不清楚能否理解复杂社交导航场景（如智能体时空关系与人类意图）。现有工作缺少系统性评估。

Method: 构建 SocialNav-SUB：面向社交机器人导航场景理解的 VQA 数据集与基准，统一框架下对 VLM、人类基线和规则基线进行比较，任务覆盖空间、时空与社会推理。

Result: 对最先进 VLM 的实验表明，最佳 VLM 与人类答案一致的概率虽令人鼓舞，但仍低于更简单的规则方法和人类共识基线。

Conclusion: 当前 VLM 在社交场景理解上存在关键差距，该基准为社交机器人导航基础模型研究提供框架，探索如何定制 VLM 以满足真实需求。

Abstract: Robot navigation in dynamic, human-centered environments requires socially-compliant decisions grounded in robust scene understanding. Recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) exhibit promising capabilities such as object recognition, common-sense reasoning, and contextual understanding, capabilities that align with the nuanced requirements of social robot navigation. However, it remains unclear whether VLMs can accurately understand complex social navigation scenes (e.g., inferring the spatial-temporal relations among agents and human intentions), which is essential for safe and socially compliant robot navigation. While some recent works have explored the use of VLMs in social robot navigation, no existing work systematically evaluates their ability to meet these necessary conditions. In this paper, we introduce the Social Navigation Scene Understanding Benchmark (SocialNav-SUB), a Visual Question Answering (VQA) dataset and benchmark designed to evaluate VLMs for scene understanding in real-world social robot navigation scenarios. SocialNav-SUB provides a unified framework for evaluating VLMs against human and rule-based baselines across VQA tasks requiring spatial, spatiotemporal, and social reasoning in social robot navigation. Through experiments with state-of-the-art VLMs, we find that while the best-performing VLM achieves an encouraging probability of agreeing with human answers, it still underperforms simpler rule-based approach and human consensus baselines, indicating critical gaps in social scene understanding of current VLMs. Our benchmark sets the stage for further research on foundation models for social robot navigation, offering a framework to explore how VLMs can be tailored to meet real-world social robot navigation needs. An overview of this paper along with the code and data can be found at https://larg.github.io/socialnav-sub.

</details>


### [125] [GPU-Accelerated Path-Dependent Marginal Information Gain for Autonomous Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40297)
*João Félix Mendes,Rodrigo Ventura,Meysam Basiri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GPU加速的路径相关边际信息增益计算方法，用深度缓冲表示历史观测，剔除祖先视角重叠区域，实现高效候选视点评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样规划器基于体积射线投射估计信息增益，计算代价高，通常假设候选视点相互独立，忽略同一路径上视点间的观测重叠，导致增益估计偏差。

Method: 用深度缓冲替代存储与合并未知体素，将候选射线投影到祖先深度缓冲以识别观测重叠并排除预期被观测区域；按深度顺序评估规划树以维持视点与优化偏航角依赖，各级候选节点和射线在GPU上并行处理。

Result: 在桌面GPU上最高118倍加速，NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX上28倍加速，与基于体素哈希图的精确边际增益误差在5-10%以内。集成到两种规划器并在三个仿真环境中评估，六个组合中有五个减少了达到95%覆盖所需时间；真实实验达到95%覆盖时间减少30%，探索终止更早。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持接近精确边际增益精度的同时显著加速视点评估，可有效提升采样式自主探索规划器的覆盖效率与终止及时性。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration demands that robots continuously evaluate candidate viewpoints based on their expected information gain and execution cost. Sampling-based planners estimate this gain by volumetric raycasting and, due to its computational cost, evaluate candidates under an assumption of mutual independence, ignoring the overlap between viewpoints along the same path. This work presents a GPU-accelerated method for computing path-dependent marginal information gain, where instead of storing and merging the observed unknown voxels along each candidate path, previous observations are represented using depth buffers. Candidate rays are projected into the depth buffers of their ancestors to identify observation overlap and exclude regions expected to be observed. The planning tree is evaluated in depth order to maintain the dependency between viewpoints and their optimized yaws, while candidate nodes and rays at each level are processed in parallel on the GPU. The proposed method stays within 5-10% of the exact marginal gain computed using voxel hash maps, with speed-ups of up to 118x on a desktop GPU and 28x on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX. The method was integrated into two sampling-based exploration planners and evaluated in three simulation environments, where marginal gain reduced the time to 95% coverage in five of the six evaluated planner-environment combinations. Real-world experiments also showed a 30% reduction in the time to 95% coverage, as well as earlier exploration termination times.

</details>


### [126] [DynaHarness: A Dynamic Physical Harness for Self-Evolving Robot Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40306)
*Haoyuan Deng,Jiebin Liu,Tengxiao Zhang,Langning Yan,Hongye Cao,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DynaHarness通过共享执行契约将语义推理与物理治理耦合，实现能力修订的自演化闭环，在LIBERO-Pro上达75.2%成功率（冻结策略仅17.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人策略提供动作先验，但长时程操作仍需语义推理与物理执行的协调。语义推理的时间尺度比物理交互更粗，且回合级失败难以定位应由哪个系统组件负责，缺乏针对性修订指导。

Method: 提出DynaHarness动态物理治理框架：慢脑提出能力与符号参数，快脑对命令进行接地、监控，拒绝未解析动作、替换能力并在需要时请求重规划。物理执行契约约束每条被接受的命令并记录执行证据（解析技能、恢复技能、冻结VLA）。失败归因定位故障并指导可复用能力或执行机制的针对性修订，配对回归检查决定接受或拒绝，形成自演化闭环。

Result: 在LIBERO-Pro上，DynaHarness在800个新采样初始状态下达75.2%，冻结策略为17.5%。相同能力库下，完整动态执行达74.0%，而名义单步重规划为63.9%。

Conclusion: 结果证明了DynaHarness作为动态物理治理框架的价值，能够治理现有能力在执行过程中的接地、监控与协调方式。

Abstract: Pretrained robot policies provide useful action priors, but long-horizon manipulation still requires coordination between semantic reasoning and physical execution. Semantic reasoning operates at a coarser timescale than physical interaction, while episode-level failures provide limited guidance on which system component should be revised. We propose DynaHarness, a dynamic physical harness that couples semantic reasoning with physical governance through a shared execution contract and turns failure evidence into validated capability revisions. To be more specific, the slow brain proposes capabilities and symbolic arguments, while the fast brain grounds and monitors commands, refuses unresolved actions, substitutes capabilities, and requests replans when needed. The physical execution contract bounds each accepted command and records execution evidence across analytic skills, recovery skills, and the frozen VLA. Failure attribution localizes faults in these records and directs targeted revisions of reusable capabilities or execution mechanisms. Paired regression checks govern admission or rejection, closing the self-evolution loop. On LIBERO-Pro, DynaHarness achieves 75.2% on 800 newly sampled initial states, compared with 17.5% for the frozen policy. With the same capability library, full dynamic execution reaches 74.0% versus 63.9% under nominal one-step replanning. This demonstrates the value of DynaHarness as a dynamic physical harness that governs how existing capabilities are grounded, monitored, and coordinated during execution. Our project page is at https://denghaoyuan123.github.io/Dynaharness_page/.

</details>


### [127] [Ego4WAM: What Matters When Scaling Egocentric Human Data for Robot Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40341)
*Zhihao Sun,Liu Liu,Xinjiang Wang,Haoyi Jiang,Wei Feng,Huiqiang Zhang,Xiaosong Jia,Zhizhong Su,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了自我中心人类数据在对齐、监督与使用策略上的不同特性如何影响机器人学习，提出统一的世界-动作模型框架Ego4WAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心人类数据可作为机器人学习的可扩展经验来源，但其在对齐度、行为覆盖和监督信息上差异较大。现有研究表明数据越多效果越好，但不清楚哪些数据属性驱动下游机器人性能提升，以及如何在整个训练流程中使用这些数据。

Method: 在统一的世界-动作模型框架下，固定模型骨干，系统解耦人-机器人对齐、数据时长、任务多样性、动作监督和数据使用策略的影响。

Result: 对齐的人类演示显著提升分布外泛化能力并降低目标任务的机器人数据需求；数据时长和任务多样性对下游能力影响不同；仅有视频监督在无动作标签时仍有效，可为后续视频-动作训练提供良好基础。通过在真实机器人和RoboDojo上的闭环策略评估验证了这些发现。

Conclusion: 不应仅将数据时长作为唯一扩展维度；Ego4WAM表明对齐、任务多样性、可用监督和使用策略共同决定自我中心人类数据对机器人学习的价值。

Abstract: Egocentric human data provides a scalable source of experience for robot learning, but varies substantially in human-robot alignment, behavioral coverage, and available supervision. Existing work shows favorable scaling with increasing human data, but it remains unclear which data properties drive downstream robot gains and how to use such data throughout the training pipeline. We present a systematic study of egocentric human data with different alignment and supervision under a unified world-action model framework. With the model backbone fixed, we disentangle the effects of human-robot alignment, data duration and task diversity, action supervision, and data usage strategies. We find that aligned human demonstrations substantially improve out-of-distribution generalization and reduce target-task robot data requirements; data duration and task diversity affect downstream capabilities differently; and video-only supervision remains effective without action labels, providing a strong foundation for subsequent video-action training. We validate these findings through closed-loop policy evaluation on both real robots and RoboDojo. Rather than treating data duration as the sole scaling axis, Ego4WAM shows how alignment, task diversity, available supervision, and usage strategy jointly shape the value of egocentric human data for robot learning.

</details>
